{[{E^{\star}_{\bar{h},\lambda}}]}[{A^{\!\star}}]={}_{\text{\sc sdp}}{\rm{H}}\overline{\textsc{e}}(h,{A^{\!\star}})+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{B^{\!\star}}\in M_{n}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})_{r}\\
{\exists k,\,B}\prec{{A^{+}_{h.k}}}\end{subarray}}{{\textsc{p}}_{{{E^{\star}_{\bar{h},\lambda}}},{A^{\!\star}},{B^{\!\star}}}}[{{B^{\!\star}}}]\;\;\;({{\textsc{p}}_{{{E^{\star}_{\bar{h},\lambda}}},{A^{\!\star}},{B^{\!\star}}}}\in\mathcal{Z}).

(2)

If
A
satisfies the SDP condition on the
h
-th row, then we have in
\mathcal{Q}^{s}_{\upsilon}(n,r)

(4.3.2)

\displaystyle{{[{F^{\star}_{\bar{h},\lambda}}]}[{A^{\!\star}}]}=

\displaystyle(-1)^{\wp({A^{\!\star}})}\sum_{k=1}^{n}{(-1)}^{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}}{\upsilon}^{g^{-}_{h}(A,k)}\Big\{{\upsilon}^{-a^{\bar{1}}_{h,k}}[{{A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h+1,k}}]

\displaystyle-{{\upsilon}}^{-a^{\bar{1}}_{h,k}}({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})\left[{a_{h+1,k}+1}\atop 2\right][{{A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h,k}+2E_{h+1,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h+1,k}}]

\displaystyle+{{\upsilon}}^{a^{\bar{0}}_{h,k}}[{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h+1,k}+1][{{A}^{\bar{0}}+E_{h+1,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,k}}]\Big\}=:{}_{\text{\sc sdp}}{\rm{H}}\overline{\textsc{f}}(h,{A^{\!\star}}).

In general, we have, for some
{{\textsc{p}}_{F^{\star}_{\bar{h},\lambda},{A^{\!\star}},{B^{\!\star}}}}\in\mathcal{Z}
,

{{[{F^{\star}_{\bar{h},\lambda}}]}[{{A^{\!\star}}}]}={}_{\text{\sc sdp}}{\rm{H}}\overline{\textsc{f}}(h,{A^{\!\star}})+({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})\text{\rm HH}\overline{\textsc{f}}(h,{A^{\!\star}})+\sum_{{B^{\!\star}}\in M_{n}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})_{r}\atop\exists k,B\prec{A}^{-}_{h,k}}{{\textsc{p}}_{F^{\star}_{\bar{h},\lambda},{A^{\!\star}},{B^{\!\star}}}}[{{B^{\!\star}}}],

where, with notation (
2.2.1
) and
A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,k}:={A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,k}-E_{h+1,k}
,

\displaystyle\text{\rm HH}\overline{\textsc{f}}(h,{A^{\!\star}})=(-1)^{\wp({A^{\!\star}})}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{k-1}(-1)^{\tilde{a}^{\bar{1}}_{h,l}}~\cdot{\upsilon}^{2(\overrightarrow{\bf r}^{l}_{h+1}{-\overrightarrow{\bf r}_{h+1}^{k-1}})+g^{-}_{h}(A,k)+a_{h+1,l}+a^{\bar{0}}_{h,k}}~\Big\{{\upsilon}^{-a_{h,k}}[{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h+1,k}+1]

\displaystyle\cdot([{A^{\overline{0},-}_{h,k}-E_{h+1,l}|{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h+1,l}}]-{[{a_{h+1,l}}]_{{\upsilon}^{2}}}[{A^{\overline{0},-}_{h,k}+E_{h+1,l}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h+1,l}}])

\displaystyle+([{{{A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h+1,l}|A^{\overline{1},-}_{h,k}+E_{h+1,l}}}]-{[{a_{h+1,l}}]_{{\upsilon}^{2}}}[{{{A}^{\bar{0}}+E_{h+1,l}|A^{\overline{1},-}_{h,k}-E_{h+1,l}}}])

\displaystyle-({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})\left[{a_{h+1,k}+1}\atop 2\right]\big([{{A}^{\bar{0}}+2E_{h+1,k}-E_{h+1,l}|A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,k}+E_{h+1,l}}]

\displaystyle-{[{a_{h+1,l}}]_{{\upsilon}^{2}}}[{{A}^{\bar{0}}+2E_{h+1,k}+E_{h+1,l}|A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,k}-E_{h+1,l}}]\big)\Big\}.

Proof.

With the SDP condition, the proof of (1) is similar to that of Lemma
4.1
(2) and is omitted.

We now prove (2). First, assume that
A
satisfies the SDP condition at
(h,k)
, for all
k\in[1,n]
with
a_{h,k}>0
. By
[
DGLW
, Th. 6.4]
(1) together with Proposition
2.4
and noting
\wp({{F^{\star}_{\bar{h},\lambda}}})=\bar{1}
, we have

(4.3.3)

\displaystyle{{[{F^{\star}_{\bar{h},\lambda}}]}[{A^{\!\star}}]}=

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\upsilon}^{-\partial(F^{\star}_{\bar{h}})-\partial({A^{\!\star}})}\Phi_{F^{\star}_{\bar{h}}}\Phi_{A^{\!\star}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{\wp({A^{\!\star}})}{\upsilon}^{-\partial(F^{\star}_{\bar{h}})-\partial({A^{\!\star}})}\sum_{{1\leq k\leq n}\atop{a_{h,k}>0}}\Big\{{(-1)}^{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}}\Phi_{({A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h+1,k})}

\displaystyle+{(-1)}^{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}+1}{q}^{-1}{[\![{a_{h+1,k}+1}]\!]}_{{q}^{2},{q}}\Phi_{({A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h,k}+2E_{h+1,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h+1,k})}

\displaystyle+{(-1)}^{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}}{q}^{a_{h,k}-1}{[\![{{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h+1,k}+1}]\!]}_{{q}}\Phi_{({A}^{\bar{0}}+E_{h+1,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,k})}\Big\}.

Let
\lambda=\mathrm{ro}(A)
. Then, by (
3.5.1
),
\partial({{F^{\star}_{\bar{h},\lambda}}})=\lambda_{h+1}
. Observe that every
\Phi_{M^{\!\star}}
occurring
on the right hand side has the same base matrix
A^{-}_{h,k}
. By (
3.5.2
) and Lemma
3.7
,
\partial({M^{\!\star}})
is given as follows:

(4.3.4)

\displaystyle\partial({A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h+1,k})

\displaystyle=\partial({A^{\!\star}})-{\overleftarrow{\bf r}}^{k}_{h}+{\overrightarrow{\bf r}}^{k}_{h+1}-a^{\bar{1}}_{h,k}+a_{h+1,k},

\displaystyle\partial({A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h,k}+2E_{h+1,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h+1,k})

\displaystyle=\partial({A^{\!\star}})-{\overleftarrow{\bf r}}^{k}_{h}+{\overrightarrow{\bf r}}^{k}_{h+1}-a^{\bar{0}}_{h+1,k}-a^{\bar{1}}_{h,k},

\displaystyle\partial({A}^{\bar{0}}+E_{h+1,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,k})

\displaystyle=\partial({A^{\!\star}})-{\overleftarrow{\bf r}}^{k}_{h}+{\overrightarrow{\bf r}}^{k}_{h+1}-a^{\bar{0}}_{h,k}+a^{\bar{1}}_{h+1,k}.

Thus, we may determine the powers of
{\upsilon}
in the coefficients of
[{M^{\!\star}}]
as follows:

(4.3.5)

\displaystyle\partial({A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h+1,k})-\partial(F^{\star}_{\bar{h},\lambda})-\partial({A^{\!\star}})=-{\overleftarrow{\bf r}}^{k}_{h}-{\overleftarrow{\bf r}}^{k}_{h+1}-a^{\bar{1}}_{h,k}=g^{-}_{h}(A,k)-a^{\bar{1}}_{h,k},

\displaystyle\partial({A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h,k}+2E_{h+1,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h+1,k})-2+2a_{h+1,k}+1-\partial({{F^{\star}_{\bar{h},\lambda}}})-\partial({A^{\!\star}})=g^{-}_{h}(A,k)-a^{\bar{1}}_{h,k},

\displaystyle\partial({A}^{\bar{0}}+E_{h+1,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,k})+2(a_{h,k}-1)+{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h+1,k}-\partial({{F^{\star}_{\bar{h},\lambda}}})-\partial({A^{\!\star}})=g^{-}_{h}(A,k)+a^{\bar{0}}_{h,k}.

Here, in the third case,
{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h+1,k}
comes from
{[\![{{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h+1,k}+1}]\!]}_{{q}}={\upsilon}^{{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h+1,k}}[{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h+1,k}+1]
.
By (
4.0.2
), we have
{[\![{a_{h+1,k}+1}]\!]}_{{q}^{2},{q}}=({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1}){\upsilon}^{2a_{h+1,k}+1}\left[a_{h+1,k}+1\atop 2\right].

Substituting gives the first formula in (2).

For the general case, the term
\text{\rm HH}\overline{\textsc{f}}
in
[
DGLW
, Th. 6.4]
(2) can be similarly standardized to obtain
HH
\overline{\textsc{f}}(h,{A^{\!\star}})
in (2), noting that the base matrices of the labelling matrices
{M^{\!\star}}
in
\text{\rm HH}\overline{\textsc{f}}
are all the same as
A^{-}_{h,k}
.
∎

We now standardise some special cases discussed in
[
DGLW
, §7]
, noting the sign change for a product of two odd elements in each case. Recall the convention that we regard a term
[{M^{\!\star}}]
as
0
if
{M^{\!\star}}\not\in M_{n}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})_{r}
.

Lemma 4.4

(cf.
[
DGLW
, Prop. 7.1]
)
.

For any given
\mu\in\Lambda(n,r-1)
,
h\in[1,n-1]
, the following formulas hold in
\mathcal{Q}^{s}_{\upsilon}(n,r)
:

\displaystyle{\rm{(1)}}

\displaystyle\quad[\mu|E_{h,h+1}]\cdot[{\mu}+E_{h+1,h}|O]=[{\mu}-E_{h+1,h+1}+E_{h+1,h}|E_{h,h+1}]+{\upsilon}^{\mu_{h+1}}[{\mu}|E_{h,h}]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-({{\upsilon}}-{\upsilon}^{-1})[{{\mu}_{h}+1}][{\mu}-E_{h+1,h+1}+E_{h,h}|E_{h+1,h+1}],

\displaystyle{\rm{(2)}}

\displaystyle\quad[{\mu}|E_{h,h+1}]\cdot[{\mu}|E_{h+1,h}]={{\upsilon}}^{{\mu}_{h+1}}[{\mu}_{h}+1][{\mu}+E_{h,h}|{O}]+[{\mu}-E_{h+1,h+1}|E_{h,h+1}+E_{h+1,h}]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-({{\upsilon}}-{\upsilon}^{-1})[{\mu}-E_{h+1,h+1}|E_{h,h}+E_{h+1,h+1}]

\displaystyle{\rm{(3)}}

\displaystyle\quad[{\mu}|E_{h+1,h}]\cdot[{\mu}+E_{h,h+1}|O]=[{\mu}-E_{h,h}+E_{h,h+1}|E_{h+1,h}]+{\upsilon}^{-\mu_{h}}[{\mu}|E_{h+1,h+1}],

\displaystyle{\rm(4)}

\displaystyle\quad[{\mu}|E_{h+1,h}]\cdot[{\mu}|E_{h,h+1}]=-[{\mu}-E_{h,h}|E_{h,h+1}+E_{h+1,h}]+{\upsilon}^{-\mu_{h}}[{\mu}_{h+1}+1][{\mu}+E_{h+1,h+1}|O].

Using the standardisation of
\phi_{A^{\star}}
, the relations in
[
DGLW
, Lem.7.2]
can be modified as follows.

Lemma 4.5
.

Let
h\in[1,n-1]
and

{\lambda}\in\Lambda(n,r-1)
. Then the following relations hold in
\mathcal{Q}^{s}_{\upsilon}(n,r)
:

\displaystyle(1)

\displaystyle\quad[{\lambda}|E_{h,h+1}]\cdot[{\lambda}|E_{h+1,h+2}]+{{\upsilon}}[{\lambda}+\alpha_{h}|E_{h+1,h+2}]\cdot[{\lambda}-\alpha_{h+1}|E_{h,h+1}]=

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt[{\lambda}+E_{h,h+1}|O]\cdot[{\lambda}+E_{h+1,h+2}|O]-{{\upsilon}}[{\lambda}+\alpha_{h}+E_{h+1,h+2}|O]\cdot[{\lambda}-\alpha_{h+1}+E_{h,h+1}|O].

\displaystyle(2)

\displaystyle\quad[\lambda|E_{h,h+1}]\cdot[\lambda+E_{h+1,h+2}|O]-{\upsilon}[\lambda+\alpha_{h}+E_{h+1,h+2}|O]\cdot[\lambda-\alpha_{h+1}|E_{h,h+1}]=

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt[{\lambda}+E_{h,h+1}|O]\cdot[{\lambda}|E_{h+1,h+2}]-{{\upsilon}}[{\lambda}+\alpha_{h}|E_{h+1,h+2}]\cdot[{\lambda}-\alpha_{h+1}+E_{h,h+1}|O].

We will prove in Corollary
7.14
, using a triangular relation, that the multiplication formulas in Lemmas
4.1
–
4.3
determine the structure of queer
q
-Schur superalgebras. More precisely, the structure constants from these formulas provide the matrix representation of the regular module
{}_{\bm{\mathcal{A}}}\bm{\mathcal{A}}
for the
\mathbb{Q}({\upsilon})
-superalgebra
\bm{\mathcal{A}}=\bm{\mathcal{Q}}^{s}_{\upsilon}(n,r)
.

5.
Long multiplication formulas in
\bm{\mathcal{Q}}^{s}_{\upsilon}(n,r)

So far, we used certain elements and relations in the Hecke-Clifford superalgebras to determine the structure of their associated queer
q
-Schur superalgebras. In other words, we have completed Step 5 in the roadmap set in the introduction. We now show that these structure can be lifted to the quantum queer supergroup
{\bm{U}}_{\!{v}}(\mathfrak{{q}}_{n})
via a formal limit process. So, we work with base changed queer
{\upsilon}
-Schur superalgebras
\bm{\mathcal{Q}}^{s}_{\upsilon}(n,r)=\mathcal{Q}^{s}_{\upsilon}(n,r)\otimes_{\mathcal{Z}}\mathbb{Q}({\upsilon})
from now on.

We first observe that, for any
{A^{\!\star}}=(A^{\bar{0}}|A^{\bar{1}})=(a^{\bar{0}}_{i,j}|a^{\bar{1}}_{i,j})\in M_{n}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})
, there is an associated square matrix
A^{\star\!\!\!\!\square}={A^{\bar{0}}\;A^{\bar{1}}\choose A^{\bar{1}}\;A^{\bar{0}}}
. By mimicking the construction in
[
BLM
]
, we introduce
the subset
M_{n}^{\pm}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})
of
M_{n}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})
consisting of
{A^{\!\star}}=(A^{\bar{0}}|A^{\bar{1}})
such that the diagonal of
A^{\bar{0}}
is 0:

(5.0.1)

M_{n}^{\pm}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2}):=\{{A^{\!\star}}\in M_{n}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})\mid A^{\star\!\!\!\!\square}\text{ has a 0 diagonal}\}.

For any
{A^{\!\star}}=({A}^{\bar{0}}|{A}^{\bar{1}})\in M_{n}^{\pm}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})
,
\bm{j}\in{\mathbb{Z}}^{n}
,
define the following “long elements” in
\bm{\mathcal{Q}}^{s}_{\upsilon}(n,r)
:

(5.0.2)

\displaystyle{{A^{\!\star}}}(\bm{j},r)=\left\{\begin{aligned} &\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot\bm{j}}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}}],\ &\mbox{if }|{A}|\leq r;\\
&0,&\mbox{otherwise}.\end{aligned}\right.

where
\lambda\centerdot\bm{j}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda_{i}j_{i}
is the dot product.

The first set of the long (element) multiplication formulas are straightforward.

Proposition 5.1
.

Let
h\in[1,n]
,
\bm{j},\bm{k}\in{\mathbb{Z}}^{n}
, and
O^{\star}:=(O|O),{A^{\!\star}}\in M_{n}^{\pm}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})
. Then, for all
r\geq|{A}|
,
the following hold in
\bm{\mathcal{Q}}^{s}_{\upsilon}(n,r)
:

(1)

{O^{\star}}(\bm{k},r)\cdot{{A^{\!\star}}}(\bm{j},r)={{\upsilon}}^{\mathrm{ro}(A)\centerdot\bm{k}}{{A^{\!\star}}}(\bm{k}+\bm{j},r);

(2)

{{A^{\!\star}}}(\bm{j},r)\cdot{O^{\star}}(\bm{k},r)={{\upsilon}}^{\mathrm{co}(A)\centerdot\bm{k}}{{A^{\!\star}}}(\bm{k}+\bm{j},r).

In particular, we have

(a)

{O^{\star}}(\bm{0},r)\cdot{{A^{\!\star}}}(\bm{j},r)={{A^{\!\star}}}(\bm{j},r)\cdot{O^{\star}}(\bm{0},r)={{A^{\!\star}}}(\bm{j},r);

(b)

{O^{\star}}(\pm\epsilon_{h},r)\cdot{{A^{\!\star}}}(\bm{j},r)={v}^{\pm\sum_{u=1}^{n}a_{h,u}}{{A^{\!\star}}}(\bm{j}\pm\epsilon_{h},r);

(c)

{{A^{\!\star}}}(\bm{j},r)\cdot{O^{\star}}(\pm\epsilon_{h},r)={v}^{\pm\sum_{u=1}^{n}a_{u,h}}{{A^{\!\star}}}(\bm{j}\pm\epsilon_{h},r).

We need the following short form of the notation in (
4.0.4
):

(5.1.1)

\displaystyle f_{h,k}^{\overline{0}}

\displaystyle=f_{h}(A,k)+{a}^{\bar{1}}_{h+1,k},\quad f_{h,k}^{\overline{1}}=\begin{cases}f_{h}(A,k)-{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h+1,k},&\text{ if }k\neq h+1;\\
f_{h}(A,h+1),&\text{ if }k=h+1,\end{cases}

\displaystyle g_{h,k}^{\bar{0}}

\displaystyle=g_{h}(A,k)-a^{\bar{1}}_{h,k},\qquad g_{h,k}^{\overline{1}}=\begin{cases}g_{h}(A,k)+{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h,k},&\text{ if }k\neq h;\\
g_{h}(A,h),&\text{ if }k=h.\end{cases}

Proposition 5.2
.

For
h\in[1,n-1]
and
{A^{\!\star}}=({A}^{\bar{0}}|{A}^{\bar{1}})=(a^{\bar{0}}_{i,j}|a^{\bar{1}}_{i,j})\in M_{n}^{\pm}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})
with base
A=(a_{i,j})
,
let
A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,k}:={A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,k}-E_{h+1,k}
and
\boxed{a}_{h,k}:=({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})\left[a_{h,k}+1\atop 2\right]
. Then, for all
r\geq|{A}|
, the following multiplication formulas hold in
\bm{\mathcal{Q}}^{s}_{\upsilon}(n,r)
:

\displaystyle(1)

\displaystyle({E_{h,h+1}}|{{O}})(\bm{0},r)\cdot{A^{\!\star}}(\bm{j},r)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k<h}{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,k}^{\overline{0}}}[{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h,k}+1](A^{\overline{0},+}_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}})(\bm{j}+\alpha_{h},r)+\sum_{h+1<k}{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,k}^{\overline{0}}}[{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h,k}+1](A^{\overline{0},+}_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}})(\bm{j},r)

\displaystyle+\frac{{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,h}^{\overline{0}}-j_{h}}}{{\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1}}\left\{({A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h+1,h}|{A}^{\bar{1}})(\bm{j}+\alpha_{h},r)-({A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h+1,h}|{A}^{\bar{1}})(\bm{j}-\alpha^{+}_{h},r)\right\}

\displaystyle+{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,h+1}^{\overline{0}}+j_{h+1}}[{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h,h+1}+1]({A}^{\bar{0}}+E_{h,h+1}|{A}^{\bar{1}})(\bm{j},r)

\displaystyle+\sum_{k<h}{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,k}^{\overline{1}}}({A}^{\bar{0}}|A^{\overline{1},+}_{h,k})(\bm{j}+\alpha_{h},r)+\sum_{h+1<k}{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,k}^{\overline{1}}}({A}^{\bar{0}}|A^{\overline{1},+}_{h,k})(\bm{j},r)

\displaystyle+{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,h}^{\overline{1}}}({A}^{\bar{0}}|A^{\overline{1},+}_{h,h})(\bm{j}-\epsilon_{h+1},r)+{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,h+1}^{\overline{1}}}({A}^{\bar{0}}|A^{\overline{1},+}_{h,h+1})(\bm{j}-\epsilon_{h+1},r)

\displaystyle-\sum_{k<h}{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,k}^{\overline{1}}}\boxed{a}_{h,k}({A}^{\bar{0}}+2E_{h,k}|A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,k})(\bm{j}+\alpha_{h},r)-\sum_{h+1<k}{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,k}^{\overline{1}}}\boxed{a}_{h,k}({A}^{\bar{0}}+2E_{h,k}|A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,k})(\bm{j},r)

\displaystyle-\frac{{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,h}^{\overline{1}}-2j_{h}}}{{\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1}}\bigg\{\frac{{\upsilon}^{-1}}{[2]}({A}^{\bar{0}}|A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,h})(\bm{j}+2\epsilon_{h}-\epsilon_{h+1},r)+\frac{{\upsilon}}{[2]}({A}^{\bar{0}}|A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,h})(\bm{j}-2\epsilon_{h}-\epsilon_{h+1},r)

\displaystyle-({A}^{\bar{0}}|A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,h})(\bm{j}-\epsilon_{h+1},r)\bigg\}-{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,h+1}^{\overline{1}}}\boxed{a}_{h,h+1}({A}^{\bar{0}}+2E_{h,h+1}|A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,h+1})(\bm{j}-\epsilon_{h+1},r),

\displaystyle(2)

\displaystyle({E_{h+1,h}}|{O})(\bm{0},r)\cdot{A^{\!\star}}(\bm{j},r)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k<h}{\upsilon}^{g_{h,k}^{\overline{0}}}[{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h+1,k}+1]({A^{\overline{0},-}_{h,k}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}})(\bm{j},r)+\sum_{h+1<k}{{\upsilon}}^{g_{h,k}^{\overline{0}}}[{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h+1,k}+1]({A^{\overline{0},-}_{h,k}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}})(\bm{j}-\alpha_{h},r)

\displaystyle+{{\upsilon}}^{g_{h,h}^{\overline{0}}+j_{h}}[{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h+1,h}+1]({{A}^{\bar{0}}+E_{h+1,h}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}})(\bm{j},r)

\displaystyle+\frac{{{\upsilon}}^{g_{h,h+1}^{\overline{0}}-j_{h+1}}}{{\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1}}\bigg\{({{A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h,h+1}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}})(\bm{j}-\alpha_{h},r)-({{A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h,h+1}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}})(\bm{j}-\alpha^{+}_{h},r)\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k<h}{{\upsilon}}^{g_{h,k}^{\overline{1}}}({{A}^{\bar{0}}}|{A^{\overline{1},-}_{h,k}})(\bm{j},r)+\hskip-5.69046pt\sum_{h+1<k}{{\upsilon}}^{g_{h,k}^{\overline{1}}}({{A}^{\bar{0}}}|{A^{\overline{1},-}_{h,k}})({\bm{j}}-\alpha_{h},r)

\displaystyle+{{\upsilon}}^{g_{h,h}^{\overline{1}}}({{A}^{\bar{0}}}|{A^{\overline{1},-}_{h,h}})({\bm{j}}+\epsilon_{h},r)+{{{\upsilon}}^{g_{h,h+1}^{\overline{1}}}}({{A}^{\bar{0}}}|{A^{\overline{1},-}_{h,h+1}})(\bm{j}-\epsilon_{h},r)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\sum_{k<h}{{\upsilon}}^{g_{h,k}^{\overline{1}}}\boxed{a}_{h+1,k}({{A}^{\bar{0}}+2E_{h+1,k}}|{A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,k}})(\bm{j},r)-\!\sum_{h+1<k}{{\upsilon}}^{g_{h,k}^{\overline{1}}}\boxed{a}_{h+1,k}({{A}^{\bar{0}}+2E_{h+1,k}}|{A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,k}})(\bm{j}-\alpha_{h},r)

\displaystyle-{{\upsilon}}^{g_{h,h}^{\overline{1}}}\boxed{a}_{h+1,h}({{A}^{\bar{0}}+2E_{h+1,h}}|{A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,h}})(\bm{j}+\epsilon_{h},r)-\frac{{{\upsilon}}^{g_{h,h+1}^{\overline{1}}-2j_{h+1}}}{{\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1}}\bigg\{\frac{{\upsilon}^{-1}}{[2]}({{A}^{\bar{0}}}|{A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,h+1}})(\bm{j}-\epsilon_{h}+2\epsilon_{h+1},r)

\displaystyle+\frac{{\upsilon}}{[2]}({{A}^{\bar{0}}}|{A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,h+1}})(\bm{j}-\epsilon_{h}-2\epsilon_{h+1},r)-({{A}^{\bar{0}}}|{A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,h+1}})(\bm{j}-\epsilon_{h},r)\bigg\}.

Proof.

We only prove (1). The proof of
(2) is similar.
By Definition (
5.0.2
) and Lemma
4.1
(2), we may expand the product on the LHS as a sum of three parts
each of which is a sum on the
h
-th row of
A^{\bar{0}}
or
A^{\bar{1}}
:

\displaystyle({E_{h,h+1}}|{O})(\bm{0},r)\cdot({{A}^{\bar{0}}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}})(\bm{j},r)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\\
\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}}[\mathrm{ro}(A+\lambda)-E_{h+1,h+1}+E_{h,h+1}|O][{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}}]={\mathcal{Y}}_{1}+{\mathcal{Y}}_{2}-{\mathcal{Y}}_{3},

where, denoting the
(h,k)
entry of a matrix
X
by
X_{h,k}
,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{Y}}_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\\
\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h}(A+\lambda,k)+{a}^{\bar{1}}_{h+1,k}}[{({A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda)}_{h,k}+1][{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda-E_{h+1,k}+E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}],

\displaystyle{\mathcal{Y}}_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\\
\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h}(A+\lambda,k)-({A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda)_{h+1,k}}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h+1,k}+E_{h,k}],

\displaystyle{\mathcal{Y}}_{3}

\displaystyle=\!\!\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\\
\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h}(A+\lambda,k)-({A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda)_{h+1,k}}({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})\left[{(A+\lambda)_{h,k}}+1\atop 2\right][{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda+2E_{h,k}|A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,k}].

Clearly,
({A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda)_{i,j}={a}^{\bar{0}}_{i,j}
for
i\neq j
and
({A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda)_{i,i}=\lambda_{i}
.
By (
4.0.4
) and (
3.6.1
),

(5.2.1)

f_{h}(A+\lambda,k)=\left\{\begin{aligned} &{{\overrightarrow{\bf r}}^{k}_{h}}-{\overrightarrow{\bf r}}^{k}_{h+1}+\lambda_{h}-\lambda_{h+1},&\mbox{ if }k<h,\\
&{{\overrightarrow{\bf r}}^{k}_{h}}-{\overrightarrow{\bf r}}^{k}_{h+1}-\lambda_{h+1},&\mbox{ if }k=h,\\
&{{\overrightarrow{\bf r}}^{k}_{h}}-{\overrightarrow{\bf r}}^{k}_{h+1},&\mbox{ if }k>h.\end{aligned}\right.

Using the notations in (
5.1.1
) and (
2.2.1
),
we have after swapping two
\Sigma
sums

(5.2.2)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{Y}}_{1}=

\displaystyle\sum_{k<h}{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,k}^{\overline{0}}}[{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h,k}+1]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\\
\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}+\lambda_{h}-\lambda_{h+1}}[A^{\overline{0},+}_{h,k}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}}]

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\\
\end{subarray}}{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,h}^{\overline{0}}}[\lambda_{h}+1]{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}-\lambda_{h+1}}[A^{\bar{0},+}_{h,h}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}}]

\displaystyle+{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,h+1}^{\overline{0}}}[{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h,h+1}+1]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\\
\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}}[A^{\bar{0},+}_{h,h+1}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}}]

\displaystyle+\sum_{k>h+1}{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,k}^{\overline{0}}}[{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h,k}+1]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\\
\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}}[A^{\overline{0},+}_{h,k}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}}]

The first and last summations have summands
(A^{\overline{0},+}_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}})(\bm{j}+{\alpha}_{h},r)
and
(A^{\overline{0},+}_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}})(\bm{j},r)
, respectively. The middle two summations correspond to the
k=h
and
k=h+1
cases.

For
k=h
, a direct calculation shows

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\\
\end{subarray}}{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,h}^{\overline{0}}}[\lambda_{h}+1]{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}-\lambda_{h+1}}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda-E_{h+1,h}+E_{h,h}|{A}^{\bar{1}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mu\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|+1)\\
\end{subarray}}{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,h}^{\overline{0}}}[\mu_{h}]{v}^{(\mu-\epsilon_{h})\centerdot{\bm{j}}-\mu_{h+1}}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\mu-E_{h+1,h}|{A}^{\bar{1}}]\;\;(\mbox{set }\;\mu=\lambda+\epsilon_{h})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{{\displaystyle\upsilon}}^{f_{h,h}^{\overline{0}}-\bm{j}_{h}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mu\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|+1)\\
\end{subarray}}\frac{{\upsilon}^{\mu\centerdot{(\bm{j}+\epsilon_{h}-\epsilon_{h+1})}}-{\upsilon}^{\mu\centerdot{(\bm{j}-\epsilon_{h}-\epsilon_{h+1})}}}{{\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1}}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\mu-E_{h+1,h}|{A}^{\bar{1}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,h}^{\overline{0}}-\bm{j}_{h}}}{{\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1}}\left\{({A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h+1,h}|{A}^{\bar{1}})(\bm{j}+{\alpha}_{h},r)-({A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h+1,h}|{A}^{\bar{1}})(\bm{j}-{\alpha}^{+}_{h},r)\right\},

while, for
k=h+1
,

\displaystyle{{\displaystyle\upsilon}}^{f_{h,h+1}^{\overline{0}}}[{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h,h+1}+1]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\\
\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda-E_{h+1,h+1}+E_{h,h+1}|{A}^{\bar{1}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{{\displaystyle\upsilon}}^{f_{h,h+1}^{\overline{0}}}[{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h,h+1}+1]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mu\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|-1)\\
\end{subarray}}{v}^{(\mu+\epsilon_{h+1})\centerdot{\bm{j}}}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\mu+E_{h,h+1}|{A}^{\bar{1}}](\mbox{set }\mu=\lambda-\epsilon_{h+1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{{\displaystyle\upsilon}}^{f_{h,h+1}^{\overline{0}}+\bm{j}_{h+1}}[{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h,h+1}+1]({A}^{\bar{0}}+E_{h,h+1}|{A}^{\bar{1}})(\bm{j},r).

Altogether shows that
\mathcal{Y}_{1}
is the first part of the multiplication formula in (1).

We now compute
\mathcal{Y}_{2}
.
Observe
({A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda)_{h+1,k}={a}^{\bar{0}}_{h+1,k}
for
k\neq h+1
and
({A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda)_{h+1,h+1}=\lambda_{h+1}
.
Similarly, by the notation in (
2.2.1
), we have after swapping two
\Sigma
sums

\displaystyle{\mathcal{Y}}_{2}=

\displaystyle\sum_{k<h}{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h}(A,k)-{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h+1,k}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\\
\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}+\lambda_{h}-\lambda_{h+1}}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+{\lambda}|A^{\overline{1},+}_{h,k}]

\displaystyle+{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h}(A,h)-{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h+1,h}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\\
\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}-\lambda_{h+1}}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda|A^{\bar{1},+}_{h,h}]

\displaystyle+{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h}(A,h+1)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\\
\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}-\lambda_{h+1}}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda|A^{\bar{1},+}_{h,h+1}]

\displaystyle+\sum_{k>h+1}{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h}(A,k)-{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h+1,k}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\\
\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda|A^{\overline{1},+}_{h,k}],

which implies the middle part of the formula in (1) after applying (
5.2.1
) and definition (
5.0.2
).

Finally, we compute
{\mathcal{Y}}_{3}=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathcal{Y}_{3,k}
, where

(5.2.3)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{Y}}_{3,k}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\\
\end{subarray}}{{\upsilon}}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}}

\displaystyle{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h}(A+\lambda,k)-({A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda)_{h+1,k}}({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})\left[{({A}+\lambda)_{h,k}}+1\atop 2\right][{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda+2E_{h,k}|A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,k}].

Since
({A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda+2E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,k}-E_{h+1,k})\in M_{n}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})_{r}
implies
{a}^{\bar{1}}_{h,k}={a}^{\bar{1}}_{h+1,k}=1
and
(A+\lambda)_{i,j}=a_{i,j}
for
i\neq j
and
(A+\lambda)_{i,i}=\lambda_{i}+1
, the computation of
\mathcal{Y}_{3,k}
depends on
k
.

If
k<h
, applying (
5.2.1
), definition (
5.0.2
) and
\boxed{a}_{h,k}=({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})\left[a_{h,k}+1\atop 2\right]
, (
5.2.3
) becomes

\displaystyle{\mathcal{Y}}_{3,k}

\displaystyle={{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,k}^{\overline{1}}}\boxed{a}_{h,k}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\\
\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}+\lambda_{h}-\lambda_{h+1}}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda+2E_{h,k}|A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,k}]

\displaystyle={{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,k}^{\overline{1}}}\boxed{a}_{h,k}({A}^{\bar{0}}+2E_{h,k}|A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,k})(\bm{j}+\epsilon_{h}-\epsilon_{h+1}).

If
k=h
, then
f_{h}(A+\lambda,h)=f_{h}(A,h)-\lambda_{h+1}
.
Let
\mu=\lambda+2\epsilon_{h}
. Then
\lambda_{h}+2=\mu_{h}
and

\left[{({A}+\lambda)_{h,h}}+1\atop 2\right]=\left[\lambda_{h}+2\atop 2\right]=\left[\mu_{h}\atop 2\right]=\frac{1}{[2]({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})^{2}}({\upsilon}^{2\mu_{h}-1}+{\upsilon}^{-2\mu_{h}+1})-\frac{1}{({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})^{2}},

which is 0 for those
\mu
with
\mu_{h}=0
or 1. Hence, by (
5.0.2
),

\displaystyle{\mathcal{Y}}_{3,h}

\displaystyle={{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,h}^{\overline{1}}}\hskip-14.22636pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mu\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|+2)\\
\end{subarray}}\hskip-14.22636pt{v}^{(\mu-2\epsilon_{h})\centerdot{\bm{j}}-\mu_{h+1}}\Big(\frac{{\upsilon}^{2\mu_{h}-1}+{\upsilon}^{-2\mu_{h}+1}}{[2]({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})}-\frac{1}{({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})}\Big)[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\mu|A^{\bar{1},\approx}_{h,h}]

\displaystyle=\frac{{{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,h}^{\overline{1}}-2j_{h}}}{{\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1}}\Big\{\frac{{\upsilon}^{-1}}{[2]}({A}^{\bar{0}}|A^{\bar{1},\approx}_{h,h})(\bm{j}+2\epsilon_{h}-\epsilon_{h+1},r)+\frac{{\upsilon}}{[2]}({A}^{\bar{0}}|A^{\bar{1},\approx}_{h,h})(\bm{j}-2\epsilon_{h}-\epsilon_{h+1},r)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-({A}^{\bar{0}}|A^{\bar{1},\approx}_{h,h})(\bm{j}-\epsilon_{h+1},r)\Big\}.

If
k=h+1
, then
f_{h}(A+\lambda,h+1)=f_{h}(A,h+1)=f^{\bar{1}}_{h,h+1}
. Thus,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{Y}}_{3,h+1}

\displaystyle={{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,h+1}^{\overline{1}}}\boxed{a}_{h,h+1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\\
\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}-\lambda_{h+1}}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda+2E_{h,h+1}|A^{\bar{1},\approx}_{h,h+1}]

\displaystyle={{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,h+1}^{\overline{1}}}\boxed{a}_{h,h+1}({A}^{\bar{0}}+2E_{h,h+1}|A^{\bar{1},\approx}_{h,h+1})(\bm{j}-\epsilon_{h+1},r).

Finally, if
k>h+1
, then (
5.2.1
),
f_{h}(A+\lambda,k)=f_{h}(A,k)
and
({A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda)_{h+1,k}=a^{\bar{0}}_{h+1,k}
. Due to (
5.0.2
),

\displaystyle{\mathcal{Y}}_{3,k}

\displaystyle={{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,k}^{\overline{1}}}\boxed{a}_{h,k}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\\
\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda+2E_{h,k}|A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,k}]={{\upsilon}}^{f_{h,k}^{\overline{1}}}\boxed{a}_{h,k}({A}^{\bar{0}}+2E_{h,k}|A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,k})(\bm{j},r).

Combining the four cases gives
\mathcal{Y}_{3}
, the last part of the formula in (1). This proves (1).
∎

We now derive long multiplication formulas in the odd case. If we regard Proposition
5.1
as the even Cartan case, then the odd Cartan case below, building on Lemma
4.2
is a bit more complicated. Moreover, we only compute the formula under the SDP condition. See Remark
5.6
below for the general case.

Proposition 5.3
.

Let

h\in[1,n]
and
{A^{\!\star}}=({A}^{\bar{0}}|{A}^{\bar{1}})=(a^{\bar{0}}_{i,j}|a^{\bar{1}}_{i,j})\in M_{n}^{\pm}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})
with base
A=(a_{i,j})
.
Assume, for any
\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)
,

A+\lambda
satisfies the SDP condition on the
h
-th row if
h<n
.
Then, for all
r\geq|{A}|
, the following multiplication formula holds in
\bm{\mathcal{Q}}^{s}_{\upsilon}(n,r)
:

\displaystyle({O}|{E_{h,h}})(\bm{0},r)\cdot{A^{\!\star}}(\bm{j},r)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k<h}{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}}+\wp({{A^{\!\star}}})}{{\upsilon}}^{d_{h}(A,k)}({A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h,k})(\bm{j}+\epsilon_{h},r)

\displaystyle\qquad+{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,h}}+\wp({{A^{\!\star}}})}{{\upsilon}}^{d_{h}(A,h)+j_{h}}({A}^{\bar{0}}|{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h,h})(\bm{j},r)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{h<k}{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}}+\wp({{A^{\!\star}}})}{{\upsilon}}^{d_{h}(A,k)}({A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h,k})(\bm{j}-\epsilon_{h},r)

\displaystyle+\sum_{k<h}{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}}+\wp({{A^{\!\star}}})}{{\upsilon}}^{d_{h}(A,k)}[a_{h,k}]_{{\upsilon}^{2}}({A}^{\bar{0}}+E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,k})(\bm{j}+\epsilon_{h},r)

\displaystyle\qquad+{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,h}}+\wp({{A^{\!\star}}})}\frac{{{\upsilon}}^{d_{h}(A,h)-j_{h}}}{{\upsilon}^{2}-{\upsilon}^{-2}}\{({A}^{\bar{0}}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,h})(\bm{j}+2\epsilon_{h},r)-({A}^{\bar{0}}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,h})(\bm{j}-2\epsilon_{h},r)\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{h<k}{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}}+\wp({{A^{\!\star}}})}{{\upsilon}}^{d_{h}(A,k)}[a_{h,k}]_{{\upsilon}^{2}}({A}^{\bar{0}}+E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,k})(\bm{j}-\epsilon_{h},r)

\displaystyle=:{\rm{H}}\overline{\textsc{k}}(h,{A^{\!\star}}(\bm{j},r)).

Proof.

Observe that for any
\mu\in\Lambda(n,r-1)
,

{(-1)}^{\wp({\mu|E_{h,h}})\cdot\wp({{A^{\!\star}}})}={(-1)}^{\wp({{A^{\!\star}}})}
.
Recalling the notation
d_{h}(A,k)
in (
4.1.9
), we have

(5.3.1)

d_{h}(A+\lambda,k)=\left\{\begin{aligned} &d_{h}(A,k)+\lambda_{h},&\mbox{ if }k<h,\\
&d_{h}(A,k),&\mbox{ if }k=h,\\
&d_{h}(A,k)-\lambda_{h},&\mbox{ if }k>h.\end{aligned}\right.

If
A+\lambda
satisfies the SDP condition on the
h
-th row for any
\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)
. By (
5.0.2
) and Lemma
4.2
(1), one can obtain

\displaystyle({O}|{E_{h,h}})(\bm{0},r)\cdot({{A}^{\bar{0}}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}})(\bm{j},r)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mu\in\Lambda(n,r-1)\\
\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\\
\mathrm{co}(\mu+E_{h,h})=\mathrm{ro}(A+\lambda)\end{subarray}}{v}^{{\mu}\centerdot{\bm{0}}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}}\cdot[{\mu}|E_{h,h}][{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}}{(-1)}^{\wp({{A^{\!\star}}})}\sum_{k=1}^{n}{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}}}{{\upsilon}}^{d_{h}(A+\lambda,k)}\{[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda-E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h,k}]

\displaystyle\quad\quad+[{(A+\lambda)}_{h,k}]_{{\upsilon}^{2}}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda+E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,k}]\}

\displaystyle={(-1)}^{\wp({{A^{\!\star}}})}({\mathcal{Y}}_{1}+{\mathcal{Y}}_{2}),

where

\displaystyle{\mathcal{Y}}_{1}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}}}{{\upsilon}}^{d_{h}(A+\lambda,k)}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda-E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h,k}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k<h}{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}}}{{\upsilon}}^{d_{h}(A,k)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}+\lambda_{h}}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda-E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h,k}]

\displaystyle+{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,h}}}{{\upsilon}}^{d_{h}(A,h)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda-E_{h,h}|{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h,h}]

\displaystyle+\sum_{k>h}{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}}}{{\upsilon}}^{d_{h}(A,k)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}-\lambda_{h}}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda-E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h,k}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k<h}{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}}}{{\upsilon}}^{d_{h}(A,k)}({A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h,k})(\bm{j}+\epsilon_{h},r)

\displaystyle+{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,h}}}{{\upsilon}}^{d_{h}(A,h)+j_{h}}({A}^{\bar{0}}|{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h,h})(\bm{j},r)

\displaystyle+\sum_{k>h}{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}}}{{\upsilon}}^{d_{h}(A,k)}({A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h,k})(\bm{j}-\epsilon_{h},r),

and

\displaystyle{\mathcal{Y}}_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}}}{{\upsilon}}^{d_{h}(A+\lambda,k)}[{(A+\lambda)}_{h,k}]_{{\upsilon}^{2}}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda+E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,k}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k<h}{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}}}{{\upsilon}}^{d_{h}(A,k)}[a_{h,k}]_{{\upsilon}^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}+\lambda_{h}}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda+E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,k}]

\displaystyle+{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,h}}}{{\upsilon}}^{d_{h}(A,h)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\end{subarray}}{{\upsilon}}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}}\frac{{\upsilon}^{2(\lambda_{h}+1)}-{\upsilon}^{-2(\lambda_{h}+1)}}{{\upsilon}^{2}-{\upsilon}^{-2}}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda+E_{h,h}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,h}](\mbox{set $\lambda+\epsilon_{h}=\mu$})

\displaystyle+\sum_{k>h}{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}}}{{\upsilon}}^{d_{h}(A,k)}[a_{h,k}]_{{\upsilon}^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\end{subarray}}{v}^{\lambda\centerdot{\bm{j}}-\lambda_{h}}[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda+E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,k}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k<h}{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}}}{{\upsilon}}^{d_{h}(A,k)}[a_{h,k}]_{{\upsilon}^{2}}({A}^{\bar{0}}+E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,k})(\bm{j}+\epsilon_{h},r)

\displaystyle+{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,h}}}\frac{{{\upsilon}}^{d_{h}(A,h)-j_{h}}}{{\upsilon}^{2}-{\upsilon}^{-2}}\{({A}^{\bar{0}}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,h})(\bm{j}+2\epsilon_{h},r)-({A}^{\bar{0}}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,h})(\bm{j}-2\epsilon_{h},r)\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k>h}{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}}}{{\upsilon}}^{d_{h}(A,k)}[a_{h,k}]_{{\upsilon}^{2}}({A}^{\bar{0}}+E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,k})(\bm{j}-\epsilon_{h},r).

In the above computation for
\mathcal{Y}_{1}
and
\mathcal{Y}_{2}
, we observe that if
({A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda-E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h,k})\in M_{n}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})
for any
1\leq k\leq n
, then
{a}^{\bar{1}}_{h,k}=0
and
{\tilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}_{h-1,k}={\tilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}_{h,k}

and if
({A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda+E_{h,k}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,k})\in M_{n}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})
for any
1\leq k\leq n
, then
{a}^{\bar{1}}_{h,k}=1
.
Putting
{\mathcal{Y}}_{1}
and
{\mathcal{Y}}_{2}
together, the proposition is proved.
∎

Applying Lemma
4.3
together with the notations in (
2.2.1
) and (
5.1.1
), we obain
the following multiplication formulas in Propositions
5.4
and
5.5
under the SDP condition. Their detailed proofs are omitted.
See Remark
5.6
for the general case.

Proposition 5.4
.

Let

h\in[1,n)
and
{A^{\!\star}}=({A}^{\bar{0}}|{A}^{\bar{1}})=(a^{\bar{0}}_{i,j}|a^{\bar{1}}_{i,j})\in M_{n}^{\pm}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})
with base
A=(a_{i,j})
.
Assume, for each
1\leq k\leq n
and
\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)
,

({A+\lambda})^{+}_{h,k}
is in
M_{n}(\mathbb{N})
and satisfies SDP condition on the
h
-th row.
Then, for all
r\geq|{A}|
, the following multiplication formula holds in
\bm{\mathcal{Q}}^{s}_{\upsilon}(n,r)
:

\displaystyle(-1)^{\wp({A^{\!\star}})}

\displaystyle({O}|{E_{h,h+1}})(\bm{0},r)\cdot{A^{\!\star}}(\bm{j},r)=

\displaystyle\sum_{k<h}{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h-1,k}}}{{\upsilon}}^{f^{\overline{0}}_{h,k}}({{A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h+1,k}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h,k}})(\bm{j}+{\alpha}_{h},r)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h-1,h}}}{{\upsilon}}^{f^{\overline{0}}_{h,h}}({{A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h+1,h}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h,h}})(\bm{j}-\epsilon_{h+1},r)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h-1,h+1}}}{{\upsilon}}^{f^{\overline{0}}_{h,h+1}+j_{h+1}}({{A}^{\bar{0}}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h,h+1}})(\bm{j},r)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k>h+1\end{subarray}}{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h-1,k}}}{{\upsilon}}^{f^{\overline{0}}_{h,k}}({{A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h+1,k}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h,k}})(\bm{j},r)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{k<h}{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h+1,k}}}{{\upsilon}}^{f^{\overline{1}}_{h,k}}[{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h,k}+1]({{A}^{\bar{0}}+E_{h,k}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h+1,k}})(\bm{j}+{\alpha}_{h},r)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h+1,h}}}\frac{{\upsilon}^{f^{\overline{1}}_{h,h}-j_{h}}}{{\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1}}\Big\{({{A}^{\bar{0}}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h+1,h}})({\bm{j}}+{\alpha}_{h},r)-({{A}^{\bar{0}}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h+1,h}})({\bm{j}}-{\alpha}_{h}^{+},r)\Big\}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h+1,h+1}}}{{\upsilon}}^{f^{\overline{1}}_{h,h+1}}[{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h,h+1}+1]({{A}^{\bar{0}}+E_{h,h+1}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h+1,h+1}})(\bm{j}-\epsilon_{h+1},r)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum_{k>h+1}{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h+1,k}}}{{\upsilon}}^{f^{\overline{1}}_{h,k}}[{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h,k}+1]({{A}^{\bar{0}}+E_{h,k}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h+1,k}})(\bm{j},r)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{k<h}{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h-1,k}}}{{\upsilon}}^{f^{\overline{0}}_{h,k}}\boxed{a}_{h,k}({{A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h+1,k}+2E_{h,k}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,k}})({\bm{j}}+{\alpha}_{h},r)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h-1,h}}}\frac{{{\upsilon}}^{f^{\overline{0}}_{h,h}-2j_{h}}}{({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})}\Big\{\frac{{\upsilon}^{-1}}{[2]}({{A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h+1,h}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,h}})(\bm{j}+2\epsilon_{h}-\epsilon_{h+1},r)

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt+

\displaystyle\frac{{\upsilon}}{[2]}({{A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h+1,h}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,h}})(\bm{j}-2\epsilon_{h}-\epsilon_{h+1},r)-({{A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h+1,h}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,h}})(\bm{j}-\epsilon_{h+1},r)\Big\}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h-1,h+1}}}{{\upsilon}}^{f^{\overline{0}}_{h,h+1}+j_{h+1}}({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})\left[{{a}_{h,h+1}+1}\atop 2\right]({{A}^{\bar{0}}+2E_{h,h+1}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,h+1}})(\bm{j},r)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum_{k>h+1}{(-1)}^{{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h-1,k}}}{{\upsilon}}^{f^{\overline{0}}_{h,k}}({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})\left[{{a}_{h,k}+1}\atop 2\right]({A^{\overline{0},+}_{h,k}+E_{h,k}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,k}})(\bm{j},r)

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle{\rm{H}}\overline{\textsc{e}}(h,{A^{\!\star}}(\bm{j},r)).

Recall
g^{-}_{h}(A,k)=-{\overleftarrow{\bf r}}^{k}_{h+1}-{\overleftarrow{\bf r}}^{k}_{h}
from (
4.2.5
). Similar to (
5.1.1
), we introduce the notations

(5.4.1)

g^{-,\overline{0}}_{h,k}=g^{-}_{h}(A,k)-{a}^{\bar{1}}_{h,k},\quad\quad g^{-,\overline{1}}_{h,k}=\left\{\begin{aligned} &g^{-}_{h}(A,k)+{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h,k},&\mbox{if }h\neq k;\\
&g^{-}_{h}(A,k),&\mbox{if }h=k.\end{aligned}\right.

Proposition 5.5
.

Let

h\in[1,n)
,
{A^{\!\star}}=({A}^{\bar{0}}|{A}^{\bar{1}})=(a^{\bar{0}}_{i,j}|a^{\bar{1}}_{i,j})\in M_{n}^{\pm}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})
with base
A=(a_{i,j})
, and
\boxed{a}_{\,i,j}:=({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})\left[{{a}_{i,j}+1}\atop 2\right]
.
Assume, for any
\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)
,

{A+\lambda}
satisfies the SDP condition on the
h
-th row,
then the following multiplication formulas hold in
\bm{\mathcal{Q}}^{s}_{\upsilon}(n,r)
for all
r\geq|{A}|
:

\displaystyle(-1)^{\wp({A^{\!\star}})}

\displaystyle({O}|{E_{h+1,h}})(\bm{0},r)\cdot{A^{\!\star}}(\bm{j},r)=

\displaystyle\sum_{k<h}{(-1)}^{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}}{{\upsilon}}^{g^{-,\overline{0}}_{h,k}}({{A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h,k}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h+1,k}})(\bm{j},r)

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt+{(-1)}^{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,h}}{{\upsilon}}^{g^{-,\overline{0}}_{h,h}+j_{h}}({{A}^{\bar{0}}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h+1,h}})(\bm{j},r)

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt+{(-1)}^{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,h+1}}{{\upsilon}}^{g^{-,\overline{0}}_{h,h+1}}({{A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h,h+1}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h+1,h+1}})(\bm{j}-\epsilon_{h},r)

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt+\sum_{k>h+1}{(-1)}^{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}}{{\upsilon}}^{g^{-,\overline{0}}_{h,k}}({{A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h,k}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h+1,k}})(\bm{j}-{\alpha}_{h}^{+},r)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{k<h}{(-1)}^{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}+1}{{\upsilon}}^{g^{-,\overline{0}}_{h,k}}\boxed{a}_{h+1,k}({A^{\overline{0},-}_{h,k}+E_{h+1,k}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h+1,k}})(\bm{j},r)

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt+{(-1)}^{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,h}+1}{{\upsilon}}^{g^{-,\overline{0}}_{h,h}+j_{h}}\boxed{a}_{h+1,h}({{A}^{\bar{0}}+2E_{h+1,h}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h+1,h}})(\bm{j},r)

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt+{(-1)}^{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,h+1}+1}

\displaystyle\frac{{{\upsilon}}^{g^{-,\overline{0}}_{h,h+1}-2j_{h+1}}}{{\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1}}\Big\{\frac{{\upsilon}^{-1}}{[2]}({{A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h,h+1}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h+1,h+1}})(\bm{j}-\epsilon_{h}+2\epsilon_{h+1},r)

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt+\frac{{\upsilon}}{[2]}({{A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h,h+1}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h+1,h+1}})(\bm{j}-\epsilon_{h}-2\epsilon_{h+1},r)

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt-({{A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h,h+1}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h+1,h+1}})(\bm{j}-\epsilon_{h},r)\Big\}

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt+\sum_{k>h+1}{(-1)}^{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}+1}{{\upsilon}}^{g^{-,\overline{0}}_{h,k}}\boxed{a}_{h+1,k}({A^{\overline{0},-}_{h,k}+E_{h+1,k}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h+1,k}})(\bm{j}-{\alpha}_{h}^{+},r)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{k<h}{(-1)}^{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}}{{\upsilon}}^{g^{-,\overline{1}}_{h,k}}[{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h+1,k}+1]({{A}^{\bar{0}}+E_{h+1,k}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,k}})(\bm{j},r)

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt+{(-1)}^{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,h}}{{\upsilon}}^{g^{-,\overline{1}}_{h,h}}[{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h+1,h}+1]({{A}^{\bar{0}}+E_{h+1,h}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,h}})(\bm{j}+\epsilon_{h},r)

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt+{(-1)}^{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,h+1}}

\displaystyle\frac{{{\upsilon}}^{g^{-,\overline{1}}_{h,h+1}-j_{h+1}}}{{\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1}}\Big\{({{A}^{\bar{0}}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,h+1}})(\bm{j}-{\alpha}_{h},r)-({{A}^{\bar{0}}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,h+1}})(\bm{j}-{\alpha}_{h}^{+},r)\Big\}

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt+\sum_{k>h+1}{(-1)}^{{{\widetilde{a}}^{\bar{1}}}_{h,k}}{{\upsilon}}^{g^{-,\overline{1}}_{h,k}}[{{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h+1,k}+1}]({{A}^{\bar{0}}+E_{h+1,k}}|{{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,k}})(\bm{j}-{\alpha}_{h}^{+},r)

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle{\rm{H}}\overline{\textsc{f}}(h,{A^{\!\star}}(\bm{j},r)).

Remarks 5.6
.

(1)
Since the general standard multiplication formulas for the odd case in Lemmas
4.2
and
4.3
contain tail parts which are not explicitly known, we are not able to show in Propositions
5.3
,
5.4
, and
5.5
the general case as a sum of a “head part” plus a “tail part”. We even do not know if such a product is a linear combination of some
{B^{\!\star}}(\bm{j},r)
!
We will prove this in the next section.

(2)
If
{A^{\!\star}}=(A|O)
, the multiplication formulas in Propositions
5.1
and
5.2
are identical to the type
A
formulas given in
[
BLM
, Lem. 5.3]
or
[
DDPW
, Th. 14.8]
.

(3) In every multiplication formula in Propositions
5.1
–
5.5
, all coefficients depend on the entries of
{A^{\!\star}}
and
\bm{j}
and are independent of
r\geq|A|.

6.
{\bm{U}}_{\!{v}}(\mathfrak{{q}}_{n})
-type generators and relations in
\bm{\mathcal{Q}}^{s}_{\upsilon}(n,r)

By the Schur–Weyl–Olshanski duality in Proposition
3.3
, the
\mathbb{Q}({\upsilon})
-superalgebra
\bm{\mathcal{Q}}_{\upsilon}(n,r)
as a homomorphic image of
{\bm{U}}_{\!{v}}(\mathfrak{{q}}_{n})
possesses a set of generators which satisfy the defining relations (QQ1)–(QQ6) in Definition
3.1
. We now use the long multiplication formulas in previous section to directly construct these generators in
\bm{\mathcal{Q}}^{s}_{\upsilon}(n,r)
and show that they satisfy (QQ1)–(QQ6). This approach in fact provides more. First, it builds the
{\bm{U}}_{\!{v}}(\mathfrak{{q}}_{n})
structure from Hecke–Clifford and queer
{\upsilon}
-Schur superalgebras. Second, this construction provides a new basis for
{\bm{U}}_{\!{v}}(\mathfrak{{q}}_{n})
and all structure constants of generators relative to the new basis.

For any
r\geq 1
, define the following elements in
\bm{\mathcal{Q}}^{s}_{\upsilon}(n,r)
: for all
i\in[1,n]
and
h\in[1,n)
,

(6.0.1)

\displaystyle G^{\pm}_{i,r}=({O}|{O})(\pm\epsilon_{i},r),\qquad X_{h,r}=({E_{h,h+1}}|{O})(\bm{0},r),\qquad Y_{h,r}=({E_{h+1,h}}|{O})(\bm{0},r),

\displaystyle G_{\bar{i},r}=({O}|{E_{i,i}})(\bm{0},r),\qquad X_{\bar{h},r}=({O}|{E_{h,h+1}})(\bm{0},r),\qquad Y_{\bar{h},r}=({O}|{E_{h+1,h}})(\bm{0},r).

Clearly, the nonzero matrices involved in these elements define certain Chevalley generators for the queer Lie superalgebra
\mathfrak{q}_{n}(\mathbb{Q})
.

Lemma 6.1
.

For any integer
h
with
1\leq h\leq n-1
, the following equations hold in
\bm{\mathcal{Q}}^{s}_{\upsilon}(n,r)
:

(1)

X_{\overline{h},r}Y_{\overline{h},r}+Y_{\overline{h},r}X_{\overline{h},r}=\frac{G_{h,r}G_{h+1,r}-G_{h,r}^{-1}G_{h+1,r}^{-1}}{{v}-{v}^{-1}}+({v}-{v}^{-1})G_{\overline{h},r}G_{\overline{h+1},r}.

(2)

X_{h,r}Y_{\overline{h},r}-Y_{\overline{h},r}X_{h,r}=G_{\overline{h},r}G_{h+1,r}^{-1}-G_{h,r}^{-1}G_{\overline{h+1},r}.

(3)

X_{\overline{h},r}Y_{h,r}-Y_{h,r}X_{\overline{h},r}=G_{h+1,r}G_{\overline{h},r}-G_{\overline{h+1},r}G_{h,r}.

Proof.

(1) By definition,

Y_{\overline{h},r}X_{\overline{h},r}=({O}|{E_{h+1,h}})(\bm{0},r)\cdot({O}|{E_{h,h+1}})(\bm{0},r)=\sum_{{\lambda},\mu\in\Lambda(n,r)}[\mu|E_{h+1,h}]\cdot[\lambda|E_{h,h+1}]

Since
[\mu|E_{h+1,h}]\cdot[\lambda|E_{h,h+1}]\neq 0
implies
\mathrm{co}(\mu|E_{h+1,h})=\mathrm{ro}(\lambda|E_{h,h+1})
or equivalently
{\lambda}=\mu
.
Applying Lemma
4.4
(4) (and (
5.0.2
)) gives

\displaystyle Y_{\overline{h},r}X_{\overline{h},r}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mu\in\Lambda(n,r-1)}(-[{\mu}-E_{h,h}|E_{h,h+1}+E_{h+1,h}]+{\upsilon}^{-\mu_{h}}[{\mu}_{h+1}+1][{\mu}+E_{h+1,h+1}|O])

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mu\in\Lambda(n,r-1)\\
\mu_{h}>0\end{subarray}}[{\mu}-E_{h,h}|E_{h,h+1}+E_{h+1,h}]+\sum_{\mu\in\Lambda(n,r-1)}{\upsilon}^{-\mu_{h}}[{\mu}_{h+1}+1][{\mu}+E_{h+1,h+1}|O]

\displaystyle=-({O}|{E_{h,h+1}+E_{h+1,h}})(\bm{0},r)+\frac{1}{{\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1}}({O^{\star}}(-\epsilon_{h}+\epsilon_{h+1},r)-{O^{\star}}(-\epsilon_{h}-\epsilon_{h+1},r)).

Here the second term is obtained by setting
{\lambda}=\mu+\epsilon_{h+1}
and noting

\sum_{\mu\in\Lambda(n,r-1)}{{{\upsilon}}^{-\mu_{h}}}[{\mu}_{h+1}+1][{\mu}+E_{h+1,h+1}|O]=\sum_{{\lambda}\in\Lambda(n,r)}{{\upsilon}}^{{-{\lambda}}_{h}}\Big(\frac{{\upsilon}^{{\lambda}_{h+1}}}{{\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1}}-\frac{{\upsilon}^{-{\lambda}_{h+1}}}{{\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1}}\Big)[{\lambda}|O].

Similarly, by Lemma
4.4
(2) and
(
5.0.2
),
we have

\displaystyle X_{\overline{h},r}Y_{\overline{h},r}

\displaystyle=(O|E_{h,h+1})(\bm{0},r)\cdot({O}|{E_{h+1,h}})(\bm{0},r)=\sum_{\mu\in\Lambda(n,r-1)}[\mu|E_{h,h+1}]\cdot[\mu|E_{h+1,h}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mu\in\Lambda(n,r-1)}{{\upsilon}}^{{\mu}_{h+1}}[{\mu}_{h}+1][{\mu}+E_{h,h}|O]+\sum_{\mu\in\Lambda(n,r-1)}[\mu-E_{h+1,h+1}|E_{h,h+1}+E_{h+1,h}]

\displaystyle\qquad\quad-\sum_{\mu\in\Lambda(n,r-1)}({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})[\mu-E_{h+1,h+1}|E_{h,h}+E_{h+1,h+1}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{{\upsilon}}-{\upsilon}^{-1}}({O}(\epsilon_{h}+\epsilon_{h+1},r)-{O}(-\epsilon_{h}+\epsilon_{h+1},r))+({O}|{E_{h,h+1}+E_{h+1,h}})(\bm{0},r)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})({O}|{E_{h,h}+E_{h+1,h+1}})(\bm{0},r).

On the other hand, observe that for any
\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-1)
the matrix
\lambda+E_{h+1,h+1}
satisfies the SDP condition on the
h
th row by Theorem
2.2
and then, by
applying Proposition
5.3
, we have

G_{\overline{h},r}G_{\overline{h+1},r}=({O}|{E_{h,h}})(\bm{0},r)({O}|{E_{h+1,h+1}})(\bm{0},r)=-({O}|{E_{h,h}+E_{h+1,h+1}})(\bm{0},r).

Putting all together, we obtain (1).

(2) Similar to the proof of (1), expanding
Y_{\overline{h},r}X_{h,r}
and applying
Lemma
4.4
(3) yields

\displaystyle Y_{\overline{h},r}X_{h,r}=({E_{h,h+1}}|{E_{h+1,h}})(\bm{0},r)+({O}|{E_{h+1,h+1}})(-\epsilon_{h},r).

Meanwhile, directly applying Proposition
5.2
(1) gives

\displaystyle X_{h,r}Y_{\overline{h},r}=({E_{h,h+1}}|{O})(\bm{0},r)({O}|{E_{h+1,h}})(\bm{0},r)

\displaystyle=({E_{h,h+1}}|{E_{h+1,h}})(\bm{0},r)+({O}|{E_{h,h}})(-\epsilon_{h+1},r).

Thus, (2) follows from Proposition
5.1
.

Finally, by Lemma
4.4
(1) and Proposition
5.2
, part (3) can be proved similarly.
∎

Lemma 6.2
.

For any integer
h
with
1\leq h\leq n-1
, the following equations hold in
\bm{\mathcal{Q}}^{s}_{\upsilon}(n,r)
:

\displaystyle{\rm(1)}\qquad

\displaystyle X_{h,r}X_{h+1,r}-{v}X_{h+1,r}X_{h,r}=X_{\overline{h},r}X_{\overline{h+1},r}+{v}X_{\overline{h+1},r}X_{\overline{h},r},

\displaystyle{\rm(2)}\qquad

\displaystyle Y_{h,r}Y_{h+1,r}-{v}Y_{h+1,r}Y_{h,r}=-(Y_{\overline{h},r}Y_{\overline{h+1},r}+{v}Y_{\overline{h+1},r}Y_{\overline{h},r}),

\displaystyle{\rm(3)}\qquad

\displaystyle X_{h,r}X_{\overline{h+1},r}-{v}X_{\overline{h+1},r}X_{h,r}=X_{\overline{h},r}X_{{h+1},r}-{v}X_{{h+1},r}X_{\overline{h},r},

\displaystyle{\rm(4)}\qquad

\displaystyle Y_{h,r}Y_{\overline{h+1},r}-{v}Y_{\overline{h+1},r}Y_{h,r}=Y_{\overline{h},r}Y_{{h+1},r}-{v}Y_{{h+1},r}Y_{\overline{h},r}.

Proof.

We first prove (1).
By definition and noting (
2.1.1
),

\displaystyle X_{\overline{h},r}X_{\overline{h+1},r}+{v}X_{\overline{h+1},r}X_{\overline{h},r}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\lambda,\mu\in\Lambda(n,r-1)}[\lambda|E_{h,h+1}][\mu|E_{h+1,h+2}]+{{\upsilon}}\sum_{\xi,\gamma\in\Lambda(n,r-1)}[\xi|E_{h+1,h+2}][\gamma|E_{h,h+1}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-1)\end{subarray}}[\lambda|E_{h,h+1}][\lambda|E_{h+1,h+2}]+{\upsilon}\sum_{\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-1)}[\lambda+\alpha_{h}|E_{h+1,h+2}][\lambda-\alpha_{h+1}|E_{h,h+1}].

With our convention in (
4.0.1
), when
\lambda_{h+1}=0
, the
(h+1)
-th entries of
\lambda+\alpha_{h}
and
\lambda-\alpha_{h+1}
are less than
0
, then

\displaystyle[\lambda+\alpha_{h}|E_{h+1,h+2}]=0,[\lambda-\alpha_{h+1}|E_{h,h+1}]=0

\displaystyle[{\lambda}+\alpha_{h}+E_{h+1,h+2}|O]=0,[{\lambda}-\alpha_{h+1}+E_{h,h+1}|O]=0.

Thus, by Lemma
4.5
(1),
we have

\displaystyle X_{\overline{h},r}X_{\overline{h+1},r}+{v}X_{\overline{h+1},r}X_{\overline{h},r}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-1)\end{subarray}}\big([{\lambda}+E_{h,h+1}|O][{\lambda}+E_{h+1,h+2}|O]

\displaystyle-{{\upsilon}}[{\lambda}+\alpha_{h}+E_{h+1,h+2}|O][{\lambda}-\alpha_{h+1}+E_{h,h+1}|O]\big).

On the other hand, using (
5.0.2
) and Lemma
4.1
(2), we obtain

\displaystyle X_{h,r}X_{h+1,r}-{v}X_{h+1,r}X_{h,r}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(E_{h,h+1}|O)(\bm{0},r)(E_{h+1,h+2}|O)(\bm{0},r)-{\upsilon}(E_{h+1,h+2}|O)(\bm{0},r)(E_{h,h+1}|O)(\bm{0},r)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\sum_{\lambda,\mu\in\Lambda(n,r-1)}[\lambda+E_{h,h+1}|O][\mu+E_{h+1,h+2}|O]}{-{\upsilon}}\sum_{\xi,\gamma\in\Lambda(n,r-1)}[\xi+E_{h+1,h+2}|O][\gamma+E_{h,h+1}|O]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-1)\end{subarray}}([{\lambda}+E_{h,h+1}|O][{\lambda}+E_{h+1,h+2}|O]-{{\upsilon}}[{\lambda}+\alpha_{h}+E_{h+1,h+2}|O][{\lambda}-\alpha_{h+1}+E_{h,h+1}|O])}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle X_{\overline{h},r}X_{\overline{h+1},r}+{v}X_{\overline{h+1},r}X_{\overline{h},r},

as desired.

We now prove (2).
First, applying Proposition
5.5
to the products
Y_{\overline{h},r}Y_{\overline{h+1},r}
and
Y_{\overline{h+1},r}Y_{\overline{h},r}
(as

(\lambda+E_{h+2,h+1})
and
(\lambda+E_{h+1,h})
satisfy the SDP condition on the
h
-th row and
(h+1)
-th row, respectively) yields

\displaystyle Y_{\overline{h},r}Y_{\overline{h+1},r}

\displaystyle=(O|E_{h+1,h})(\bm{0},r)(O|E_{h+2,h+1})(\bm{0},r)=-(O|E_{h+1,h}+E_{h+2,h+1})(\bm{0},r),

\displaystyle Y_{\overline{h+1},r}Y_{\overline{h},r}

\displaystyle=(E_{h+2,h}|O)(\bm{0},r)+{\upsilon}^{-1}(O|E_{h+1,h}+E_{h+2,h+1})(\bm{0},r).

Thus,
-(Y_{\overline{h},r}Y_{\overline{h+1},r}+{v}Y_{\overline{h+1},r}Y_{\overline{h},r})=-{\upsilon}(E_{h+2,h}|O)(\bm{0},r).

Second, applying Proposition
5.2
(2) gives

\displaystyle Y_{h,r}Y_{h+1,r}

\displaystyle=(E_{h+1,h}|O)(\bm{0},r)(E_{h+2,h+1}|O)(\bm{0},r)=(E_{h+1,h}+E_{h+2,h+1})(\bm{0},r),

\displaystyle Y_{h+1,r}Y_{h,r}

\displaystyle=(E_{h+2,h}|O)(\bm{0},r)+{\upsilon}^{-1}(E_{h+1,h}+E_{h+2,h+1})(\bm{0},r).

Thus,
Y_{h,r}Y_{h+1,r}-{v}Y_{h+1,r}Y_{h,r}=-{\upsilon}(E_{h+2,h}|O)(\bm{0},r)=-(Y_{\overline{h},r}Y_{\overline{h+1},r}+{v}Y_{\overline{h+1},r}Y_{\overline{h},r})
, as desired.

Finally, similar to the proof of formula (1), using (
5.0.2
) and Lemma
4.5
(2), we can prove (3). Meanwhile, similar to the proof of (2),
(\lambda+E_{h+2,h+1})
satisfies SDP condition on the
h
-th row and
(\lambda+E_{h+1,h})
satisfies SDP condition on the
(h+1)
-th row, then applying Proposition
5.2
(2) and Proposition
5.5
proves (4).
∎

Theorem 6.3
.

For each
r\geq 1
, there is a superalgebra homomorphism
\bm{\xi}_{n,r}:{\bm{U}}_{\!{v}}(\mathfrak{{q}}_{n})\to\bm{\mathcal{Q}}^{s}_{\upsilon}(n,r)
defined by

{{\mathsf{E}}}_{j}\mapsto X_{j,r},\;{{\mathsf{E}}}_{\overline{j}}\mapsto X_{\overline{j},r},\;{{\mathsf{F}}}_{j}\mapsto Y_{j,r},\;{{\mathsf{F}}}_{\overline{j}}\mapsto Y_{\overline{j},r},\;{{\mathsf{K}}}_{i}^{\pm 1}\mapsto G_{i,r}^{\pm 1},\;{{\mathsf{K}}}_{\overline{i}}\mapsto G_{\overline{i},r},

for all
1\leq i\leq n,1\leq j\leq n-1
.

Proof.

We need to verify all the relations (QQ1)–(QQ6) in Definition
3.1
with
\mathsf{K}_{i}^{\pm 1},\mathsf{E}_{j},\mathsf{F}_{j},\mathsf{K}_{\bar{i}},\mathsf{E}_{\bar{j}},\mathsf{F}_{\bar{j}}
replaced by
G_{i,r}^{\pm 1},X_{j,r},Y_{j,r},G_{\overline{i},r},X_{\overline{j},r},Y_{\overline{j},r},

respectively. By Remark
5.6
(2), relations involving only even generators (called even relations below) can be checked as in the
q
-Schur algebra case.
It suffices to check all the relations involving some odd generators.

For the non-even relations in (QQ1)
,
G_{i,r}G_{\bar{j},r}=G_{\bar{j},r}G_{i,r}
follows from Proposition
5.1
(a).

By Theorem
2.2
, any diagonal matrix statisfies SDP condition at any non-zero diagonal entry. Thus, by Proposition
5.3
with
A=(O|E_{j,j})
so
\tilde{a}_{i,i}^{\bar{1}}=\begin{cases}0,&\text{ if }i<j;\\
1,&\text{ if }i>j,\end{cases}
we have, for
i,j\in[1,n]
,

\displaystyle({O}|{E_{i,i}})(\bm{0},r)({O}|{E_{j,j}})(\bm{0},r)=\begin{cases}-({O}|{E_{j,j}+E_{i,i}})(\bm{0},r),&\text{ if }i<j;\\
({O}|{E_{j,j}+E_{i,i}})(\bm{0},r),&\text{ if }i>j;\\
\frac{1}{({\upsilon}^{2}-{\upsilon}^{-2})}\Big(({O}|{O})(2\epsilon_{j},r)-({O}|{O})(-2\epsilon_{j},r)\Big),&\text{ if }i=j.\end{cases}.

Hence,

G_{\overline{i},r}G_{\overline{j},r}+G_{\overline{j},r}G_{\overline{i},r}=0,~G_{\overline{k},r}^{2}=\frac{G_{k,r}^{2}-G_{k,r}^{-2}}{{v}^{2}-{v}^{-2}},

proving (QQ1).

For the non-even relations in (QQ2)
, all follow from Proposition
5.1
.

For the non-even relations in (QQ3)
, we divide the 12 relations into four subsets
\mathcal{R}_{i}
(
1\leq i\leq 4
), where subset
\mathcal{R}_{i}
consists of the two relations in the
i
-th line and the
i
-th relation in the last line of (QQ3).

By Theorem
2.2
,
for any
\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-1)
and
i\in[1,n]
,
j\in[1,n)
,
E_{i,i}+\lambda
,
E_{j,j+1}+\lambda

E_{j+1,j}+\lambda
satisfy the SDP condition at every non-zero entry on the
i
-th row. So, in the following computations, Propositions
5.3
,
5.4
and
5.5
are all applicable.

Case
\mathcal{R}_{1}
.
We apply Proposition
5.3
to compute the following product:

G_{\overline{i},r}X_{j,r}=({O}|{E_{i,i}})(\bm{0},r)({E_{j,j+1}}|{O})(\bm{0},r)=\begin{cases}{\upsilon}({E_{i,i+1}}|{E_{i,i}})(\bm{0},r)+({O}|{E_{i,i+1}})(-\epsilon_{i},r),&\text{ if }j=i;\\
({E_{j,j+1}}|{E_{i,i}})(\bm{0},r),&\text{ if }j\neq i.\end{cases}

Also, by Proposition
5.2
,
X_{j,r}G_{\overline{i},r}=\begin{cases}{v}({E_{i-1,i}}|{E_{i,i}})(\bm{0},r)+({O}|{E_{i-1,i}})(-\epsilon_{i},r),&\text{ if }j=i-1;\\
({E_{j,j+1}}|{E_{i,i}})(\bm{0},r),&\text{ if }j\neq i-1.\end{cases}
Thus, the three relations in
\mathcal{R}_{1}
follow.

Case
\mathcal{R}_{2}
.
Symmetrically, the three relations in
\mathcal{R}_{2}
follow from the multiplication formulas:

G_{\overline{i},r}Y_{j,r}=\begin{cases}{{\upsilon}}^{-1}({E_{i,i-1}}|{E_{i,i}})(\bm{0},r)+({O}|{E_{i,i-1}})(\epsilon_{i},r),&\text{if }j=i-1;\\
({E_{j+1,j}}|{E_{j,j}})(\bm{0},r),&\text{if }j\neq i-1.\end{cases}

Y_{j,r}G_{\overline{i},r}=({E_{j+1,j}}|{O})(\bm{0},r)({O}|{E_{i,i}})(\bm{0},r)=\begin{cases}{\upsilon}^{-1}({E_{i+1,i}}|{E_{i,i}})(\bm{0},r)+({O}|{E_{i+1,i}})(\epsilon_{i},r),&\text{if }j=i;\\
({E_{j+1,j}}|{E_{i,i}})(\bm{0},r),&\text{ if }j\neq i.\\
\end{cases}

Case
\mathcal{R}_{3}
.
By
Proposition
5.3
, we have

G_{\overline{i},r}X_{\overline{j},r}=\begin{cases}({O}|{E_{j,j+1}+E_{i,i}})(\bm{0},r),&\mbox{if }j<i;\\
-{\upsilon}({O}|{E_{i,i}+E_{i,i+1}})(\bm{0},r)+({E_{i,i+1}}|{O})(-\epsilon_{i},r),&\text{if }j=i;\\
-({O}|{E_{j,j+1}+E_{i,i}})(\bm{0},r),&\mbox{if }j>i;\end{cases}

while, by Proposition
5.4
,

X_{\overline{j},r}G_{\overline{i},r}=\begin{cases}-({O}|{E_{i,i}+E_{j,j+1}})(\bm{0},r).&\text{if }j<i-1;\\
-{\upsilon}({O}|{E_{i,i}+E_{i-1,i}})(\bm{0},r)+({E_{i-1,i}}|{O})(-\epsilon_{i},r),&\text{if }j=i-1;\\
({O}|{E_{i,i}+{E_{j,j+1}}})(\bm{0},r).&\text{if }j\geq i.\\
\end{cases}

Thus, the three relations in
\mathcal{R}_{3}
follow.

Case
\mathcal{R}_{4}
.
The remaining three relations in
\mathcal{R}_{4}
can be proved similarly, noting

G_{\overline{i},r}Y_{\overline{j},r}=\begin{cases}({O}|{E_{j+1,j}+E_{i,i}})(\bm{0},r),&\mbox{if }j<i-1;\\
{\upsilon}^{-1}({O}|{E_{i,i-1}+E_{i,i}})(\bm{0},r)+({E_{i,i-1}}|{O})(\epsilon_{i},r),&\text{if }j=i-1;\\
-({O}|{E_{j+1,j}+E_{i,i}})(\bm{0},r),&\mbox{if }j\geq i;\end{cases}

Y_{\overline{j},r}G_{\overline{i},r}=\begin{cases}-({O}|{E_{j+1,j}+E_{i,i}})(\bm{0},r),&\mbox{if }j<i;\\
-{\upsilon}^{-1}({O}|{E_{i,i}+E_{i+1,i}})(\bm{0},r)+({E_{i+1,i}}|{O})(\epsilon_{i},r),&\text{if }j=i;\\
({O}|{E_{j+1,j}+E_{i,i}})(\bm{0},r),&\mbox{if }j>i.\end{cases}

This completes checking (QQ3).

For the non-even relations in (QQ4)
, the
i=j
case is done on Lemma
6.1
. We now prove the
i\neq j
case.
By Proposition
5.4
,

X_{\bar{i},r}Y_{\overline{j},r}=({O}|{E_{i,i+1}})(\bm{0},r)({O}|{E_{j+1,j}})(\bm{0},r)=\begin{cases}-(O|E_{i,i+1}+E_{j+1,j})(\bm{0},r),&\text{if }i<j;\\
\;\;(O|E_{i,i+1}+E_{j+1,j})(\bm{0},r),&\text{if }i>j,\end{cases}

which is the same as
-Y_{\overline{j},r}X_{\bar{i},r}
, by Proposition
5.5
. Here the required SDP condition can be checked easily.
Similarly,

X_{i,r}Y_{\overline{j},r}=({E_{i,i+1}}|{O})(\bm{0},r)({O}|{E_{j+1,j}})(\bm{0},r)=(E_{i,i+1}|E_{j+1,j})(\bm{j},r)=Y_{\overline{j},r}X_{i,r}

and

X_{\bar{i},r}Y_{{j},r}=(E_{j+1,j}|E_{i,i+1})(\bm{0},r)=Y_{{j},r}X_{\bar{i},r}
, proving (QQ4).

For the non-even relations (QQ5)
, we first prove the two relations with square terms. Recall the notation for
\pm
-matrices defined in (
2.2.2
).
Since, by Theorem
2.2
,
for any
\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-1)
and
k\in[1,n]
,

{(E_{i,i+1}+\lambda)}^{+}_{i,k}\in M_{n}(\mathbb{N})
forces
k=i+1
and
{(E_{i,i+1}+\lambda)}^{+}_{i,i+1}

satisfies the SDP condition on the
i
-th row. Thus, applying Propositions
5.4
and
5.2
(1) yields

\displaystyle X_{\overline{i},r}^{2}

\displaystyle=({O}|{E_{i,i+1}})(\bm{0},r)\cdot({O}|{E_{i,i+1}})(\bm{0},r)=-({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})({2E_{i,i+1}}|{O})(\bm{0},r);

\displaystyle X_{i,r}^{2}

\displaystyle=[2]({2E_{i,i+1}}|{O})(\bm{0},r)=({\upsilon}+{\upsilon}^{-1})({2E_{i,i+1}}|{O})(\bm{0},r).

Hence,
X_{\overline{i},r}^{2}=-\frac{{\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1}}{{\upsilon}+{\upsilon}^{-1}}X_{i,r}^{2}.

Similarly, since
E_{i+1,i}+\lambda
(
\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-1)
) satisfies the SDP condition on the
i
-th row, by Propositions
5.2
(2) and
5.5
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{{v}-{v}^{-1}}{{v}+{v}^{-1}}Y_{i,r}^{2}

\displaystyle=({v}-{v}^{-1})({2E_{i+1,i}}|{O})(\bm{0},r)=Y_{\overline{i},r}^{2}.

For the relations in the second line of (QQ5), applying Propositions
5.2
and
5.4
, we obtain, for all
i,j\in[1,n]
,
(recall
X_{i,r}=({E_{i,i+1}}|{O})(\bm{0},r)
and
X_{\overline{j},r}=({O}|{E_{j,j+1}})(\bm{0},r)
.)

(6.3.1)

\displaystyle X_{i,r}X_{\overline{j},r}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}({E_{i,i+1}}|{E_{j,j+1}})(\bm{0},r),&\text{if }j\neq i+1;\\
{\upsilon}^{-1}({E_{i,i+1}}|{E_{j,j+1}})(\bm{0},r)+{(O|E_{i,i+2})({\bf 0},r)},&\text{if }j=i+1,\end{cases}

(6.3.2)

\displaystyle X_{\overline{j},r}X_{i,r}

\displaystyle=({E_{i,i+1}}|{E_{j,j+1}})(\bm{0},r),\;\;\text{if }j\neq i-1.

Hence,
X_{i,r}X_{\overline{j},r}-X_{\overline{j},r}X_{i,r}=0
for all
i,j
with
|i-j|\neq 1
. The proof for the
Y
case is similar.

Also, for
|i-j|>1
and
{\lambda}
, the +-matrix
(E_{j,j+1}+{\lambda})^{+}_{i,k}
(resp.,
(E_{i,i+1}+{\lambda})^{+}_{j,k}
) satisfies SDP condition on
i
-th (resp.,
j
-th row). By Proposition
5.4
,

X_{\bar{i},r}X_{\overline{j},r}=({O}|{E_{i,i+1}})(\bm{0},r)({O}|{E_{j,j+1}})(\bm{0},r)=\begin{cases}\;\;(O|E_{i,i+1}+E_{j,j+1})(\bm{0},r),&\text{if }i<j;\\
-(O|E_{i,i+1}+E_{j,j+1})(\bm{0},r),&\text{if }i>j.\end{cases}

Hence,
X_{\bar{i},r}X_{\overline{j},r}=-X_{\overline{j},r}X_{\bar{i},r}.
A similar argument shows
Y_{\bar{i},r}Y_{\overline{j},r}=-Y_{\overline{j},r}Y_{\bar{i},r}
.

The last four relations of (QQ5) follows from Lemma
6.2
, completing (QQ5) checking.

Finally, we prove the non-even relations in (QQ6),
we only prove the
j=i-1
case.
The
j=i+1
case can be proved similarly. After shiftting indices, we first prove

(6.3.3)

\displaystyle X_{i+1,r}^{2}X_{\overline{i},r}-({v}+{v}^{-1})X_{i+1,r}\boxed{X_{\overline{i},r}X_{i+1,r}}+X_{\overline{i},r}X_{i+1,r}^{2}=0,

Since the SDP condition does not hold for the boxed product, we modify the left hand side slightly. Recall the second relation in line four of (QQ5):

\displaystyle X_{i,r}X_{\overline{i+1},r}-{v}X_{\overline{i+1},r}X_{i,r}=X_{\overline{i},r}X_{i+1,r}-{v}X_{i+1,r}X_{\overline{i},r}.

Thus, the left hand side of (
6.3.3
) becomes

LHS

\displaystyle=X_{i+1,r}^{2}X_{\overline{i},r}-{\upsilon}^{-1}X_{i+1,r}X_{\overline{i},r}X_{i+1,r}+X_{\overline{i},r}X_{i+1,r}^{2}-{\upsilon}X_{i+1,r}X_{\overline{i},r}X_{i+1,r}

\displaystyle=-{v}^{-1}X_{i+1,r}(X_{\overline{i},r}X_{i+1,r}-{v}X_{i+1,r}X_{\overline{i},r})+(X_{\overline{i},r}X_{i+1,r}-{v}X_{i+1,r}X_{\overline{i},r})X_{i+1,r}

\displaystyle=-{v}^{-1}X_{i+1,r}X_{i,r}X_{\overline{i+1},r}+X_{i+1,r}X_{\overline{i+1},r}X_{i,r}+X_{i,r}X_{\overline{i+1},r}X_{i+1,r}-{v}X_{\overline{i+1},r}X_{i,r}X_{i+1,r}.

Applying (
6.3.1
), (
6.3.2
), and Proposition
5.2
(1),
we obtain

\displaystyle X_{i+1,r}

\displaystyle(X_{i,r}X_{\overline{i+1},r})\overset{\eqref{XiXjbar}}{=}({E_{i+1,i+2}}|{O})(\bm{0},r)\cdot\Big({\upsilon}^{-1}({E_{i,i+1}}|{E_{i+1,i+2}})(\bm{0},r)+{(O|E_{i,i+2})({\bf 0},r)}\Big)

\displaystyle={\upsilon}^{-1}({E_{i,i+1}+E_{i+1,i+2}}|{E_{i+1,i+2}})(\bm{0},r)+({E_{i+1,i+2}}|{E_{i,i+2}})(\bm{0},r),

\displaystyle X_{i+1,r}

\displaystyle(X_{\overline{i+1},r}X_{i,r})\overset{\eqref{XjbarXi}}{=}({E_{i+1,i+2}}|{O})(\bm{0},r)\cdot({E_{i,i+1}}|{E_{i+1,i+2}})(\bm{0},r)

\displaystyle=({E_{i,i+1}+E_{i+1,i+2}}|{E_{i+1,i+2}})(\bm{0},r),

\displaystyle X_{i,r}

\displaystyle(X_{\overline{i+1},r}X_{i+1,r})\overset{\eqref{XjbarXi}}{=}({E_{i,i+1}}|{O})(\bm{0},r)\cdot({E_{i+1,i+2}}|{E_{i+1,i+2}})(\bm{0},r)

\displaystyle={\upsilon}^{-2}({E_{i+1,i+2}+E_{i,i+1}}|{E_{i+1,i+2}})(\bm{0},r)+{v}({E_{i,i+2}}|{E_{i+1,i+2}})(\bm{0},r)+{\upsilon}^{-1}({E_{i+1,i+2}}|{E_{i,i+2}})(\bm{0},r),

and, by Propositions
5.2
(1) and
5.4
,

\displaystyle X_{\overline{i+1},r}

\displaystyle(X_{i,r}X_{i+1,r})=({O}|{E_{i+1,i+2}})(\bm{0},r)\cdot\Big({\upsilon}^{-1}({E_{i,i+1}+E_{i+1,i+2}}|{O})(\bm{0},r)+({E_{i,i+2}}|{O})(\bm{0},r)\Big)

\displaystyle={\upsilon}^{-1}({E_{i+1,i+2}+E_{i,i+1}}|{E_{i+1,i+2}})(\bm{0},r)+({E_{i,i+2}}|{E_{i+1,i+2}})(\bm{0},r).

Substituting gives LHS
=0
, proving (
6.3.3
).

The
Y
case can be proved symmetrically by using Proposition
5.5
because for any
\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r)
with
r>0
,
we have
d_{A}=1
when
A=\lambda+E_{i+1,i}
or
\lambda+2E_{i+1,i}
and then the SDP condition applies to all cases due to Theorem
2.2
.

This completes the proof of the theorem.∎

The first application of the theorem is to answer the question raised in Remark
5.6
(2).
Let

(6.3.4)

\mathscr{G}_{n,\bullet}=\{G_{i,\bullet},G_{i,\bullet}^{-1},G_{\overline{i},\bullet},X_{j,\bullet},X_{\overline{j},\bullet},Y_{j,\bullet},Y_{\overline{j},\bullet}\ \bm{|}\ 1\leq i\leq n,\ 1\leq j\leq n-1\}.

Then, for every
r\geq 1
,
\mathscr{G}_{n,r}=\{G_{i,r},G_{i,r}^{-1},G_{\overline{i},r},\ldots\}
is a subset of
\bm{\mathcal{Q}}^{s}_{\upsilon}(n,r)
.

Corollary 6.4
.

For any
{A^{\!\star}}\in M_{n}^{\pm}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})
with base
A

and any
Z_{\bullet}\in\mathscr{G}_{n,\bullet}
,
there exist
{{\textsc{p}}_{{B^{\!\star}},\bm{j}}(Z_{\bullet},{A^{\!\star}})}\in\mathbb{Q}({v})
,
for some
{B^{\!\star}}\in M_{n}^{\pm}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})
and
\bm{j}\in{\mathbb{Z}}^{n}
,
such that, for all
r\geq|A|
, the following holds in
\bm{\mathcal{Q}}^{s}_{\upsilon}(n,r)

(6.4.1)

\displaystyle Z_{r}\cdot{{A^{\!\star}}}(\bm{0},r)

\displaystyle=\sum_{{B^{\!\star}},\bm{j}}{{\textsc{p}}_{{B^{\!\star}},\bm{j}}(Z_{\bullet},{A^{\!\star}})}{{B^{\!\star}}}(\bm{j},r).

Proof.

If
Z_{r}
is even (i.e.,
Z_{r}\in\{G_{i,r},G_{i,r}^{-1},X_{j,r},Y_{j,r}\}
) or
Z_{r}=G_{\bar{n},r}
, the assertion follows from Propositions
5.1
,
5.2
, and
5.3
(the
h=n
case).
If
Z_{r}
is an odd element in

\{X_{\bar{j},r},Y_{\bar{j},r},G_{\bar{j},r}\mid 1\leq j<n\},

then, by the theorem and Remark
3.2
, the existence of the expression can be proved by an induction on
n
down from
n-1
to 1.
∎

The next result continues to address Remark
5.6
(2), writing the general case in Propositions
5.3
,
5.4
, and
5.5
as a sum of a head part and a tail part.
Recall the head parts
{\rm{H}}\overline{\textsc{k}}(h,{A^{\!\star}}(\bm{j},r))
,
{\rm{H}}\overline{\textsc{e}}(h,{A^{\!\star}}(\bm{j},r))
, and
{\rm{H}}\overline{\textsc{f}}(h,{A^{\!\star}}(\bm{j},r))
introduced in Propositions
5.3
,
5.4
, and
5.5
, respectively.

Corollary 6.5
.

Let

h\in[1,n)
,
i\in[1,n]
, and
{A^{\!\star}}\in M_{n}^{\pm}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})
with base matrix
A
.
Then, for all
r\geq|A|
, the following multiplication formulas hold in
\bm{\mathcal{Q}}^{s}_{\upsilon}(n,r)
:

(6.5.1)

\displaystyle G_{\overline{i},r}\cdot{{A^{\!\star}}}(\bm{0},r)={\rm{H}}\overline{\textsc{k}}(i,{A^{\!\star}}(\bm{0},r))+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{B^{\!\star}},\bm{j},\\
B\prec A\end{subarray}}{{\textsc{p}}_{{B^{\!\star}},\bm{j}}(G_{\overline{i},\bullet},{A^{\!\star}})}{{B^{\!\star}}}(\bm{j},r),

(6.5.2)

\displaystyle X_{\overline{h},r}\cdot{{A^{\!\star}}}(\bm{0},r)={\rm{H}}\overline{\textsc{e}}(h,{A^{\!\star}}(\bm{0},r))+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{B^{\!\star}},\bm{j},\\
\exists k,B\prec{A}^{+}_{h,k}\end{subarray}}{{\textsc{p}}_{{B^{\!\star}},\bm{j}}(X_{\overline{h},\bullet},{A^{\!\star}})}{{B^{\!\star}}}(\bm{j},r),

(6.5.3)

\displaystyle Y_{\overline{h},r}\cdot{{A^{\!\star}}}(\bm{0},r)={\rm{H}}\overline{\textsc{f}}(h,{A^{\!\star}}(\bm{0},r))+({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1}){\rm{HH}}\overline{\textsc{f}}(h,{A^{\!\star}}(\bm{0},r))

\displaystyle\hskip 216.81pt+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{B^{\!\star}},\bm{j},\\
\exists k,B\prec{A}^{-}_{h,k}\end{subarray}}{{\textsc{p}}_{{B^{\!\star}},\bm{j}}(Y_{\overline{h},\bullet},{A^{\!\star}})}{{B^{\!\star}}}(\bm{j},r),

where
{B^{\!\star}}\in M_{n}^{\pm}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})
with base
B
,
\bm{j}\in\mathbb{Z}^{n}
and
{\rm{HH}}\overline{\textsc{f}}(h,{A^{\!\star}}(\bm{0},r))
is a
\mathbb{Q}({\upsilon})
-linear combination of
{M^{\!\star}}(\bm{j}^{\prime},r)
with coefficients independent of
r
with
{M^{\!\star}}\in M_{n}^{\pm}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})
being one of the following matrices for
1\leq l<k\leq n
(
\dot{\delta}_{i,j}=1-\delta_{i,j}
):

(6.5.4)

\displaystyle({A}^{\bar{0}}-\dot{\delta}_{h,k}E_{h,k}+\dot{\delta}_{h+1,k}E_{h+1,k}\mp\dot{\delta}_{h+1,l}E_{h+1,l}|{A}^{\bar{1}}\pm E_{h+1,l}),

\displaystyle({A}^{\bar{0}}\mp\dot{\delta}_{h+1,l}E_{h+1,l}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,k}+E_{h+1,k}\pm E_{h+1,l}),

\displaystyle({A}^{\bar{0}}+\dot{\delta}_{h+1,k}2E_{h+1,k}\mp\dot{\delta}_{h+1,l}E_{h+1,l}|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h,k}-E_{h+1,k}\pm E_{h+1,l}).

Proof.

We only prove (
6.5.3
). The proofs for other two are similar (and simpler).

We first expand the left hand side of (
6.5.3
) and apply Proposition
4.3
(2):

(6.5.5)

\displaystyle Y_{\overline{h},r}\cdot{{A^{\!\star}}}(\bm{j},r)=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\\
\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\end{subarray}}[{\lambda}-\epsilon_{h}+\mathrm{ro}(A)|E_{h+1,h}]\cdot[{A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\\
\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\end{subarray}}\Big\{{}_{\text{\sc sdp}}{\rm{H}}\overline{\textsc{f}}(h,({A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}}))+({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})\text{\rm HH}\overline{\textsc{f}}(h,({A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}}))

\displaystyle+\sum_{{B^{\!\star}}\in M_{n}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})_{r}\atop\exists k,B\prec{(\lambda+A)}^{-}_{h,k}\approx A^{-}_{h,k}}{{\textsc{p}}_{F^{\star}_{\bar{h},{\lambda}+\mathrm{ro}(A)},A^{\star}_{\lambda},{B^{\!\star}}}}[{{B^{\!\star}}}]\Big\},

where
A^{\star}_{\lambda}=({A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}})
.

By Proposition
5.5
, we have

(6.5.6)

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\\
\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\end{subarray}}{}_{\text{\sc sdp}}{\rm{H}}\overline{\textsc{f}}(h,({A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}}))\\
={\rm{H}}\overline{\textsc{f}}(h,{A^{\!\star}}({\bf 0},r)).

On the other hand, we claim that

(6.5.7)

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\end{subarray}}({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})\text{\rm HH}\overline{\textsc{f}}(h,({A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}}))=:({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1}){\rm{HH}}\overline{\textsc{f}}(h,{A^{\!\star}}(\bm{0},r))

is a linear combination of
{M^{\!\star}}(\bm{j}^{\prime},r)
with coefficients independent of
r
with
{M^{\!\star}}\in M_{n}^{\pm}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})
being one of the matrices in (
6.5.4
).

Indeed, by Lemma
4.3
(2), the LHS of (
6.5.7
) has the form

({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})(-1)^{\wp({A^{\!\star}})}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l<k}(\clubsuit)_{h,k,l},

where, putting

\tau_{h,k,l}({A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}}):=2(\overrightarrow{\bf r}^{l}_{h+1}(A+\lambda){-\overrightarrow{\bf r}_{h+1}^{k-1}(A+\lambda)})+g^{-}_{h}((A+\lambda),k)+(A+\lambda)_{h+1,l}
,

(6.5.8)

\displaystyle(\clubsuit)_{h,k,l}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\end{subarray}}\bigg\{(-1)^{\widetilde{a}^{\bar{1}}_{h,l}}{\upsilon}^{\tau_{h,k,l}({A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}})+(A^{\bar{0}}+\lambda)_{h,k}}\Big({\upsilon}^{-(A+\lambda)_{h,k}}[{(A^{\bar{0}}+\lambda)}_{h+1,k}+1]\cdot

\displaystyle([{A^{\overline{0},-}_{h,k}-E_{h+1,l}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h+1,l}}]-{[{(A+\lambda)_{h+1,l}}]_{{\upsilon}^{2}}}[{A^{\overline{0},-}_{h,k}+E_{h+1,l}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h+1,l}}])

\displaystyle+([{{{A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h+1,l}+\lambda|A^{\overline{1},-}_{h,k}+E_{h+1,l}}}]-{[{(A+\lambda)_{h+1,l}}]_{{\upsilon}^{2}}}[{{{A}^{\bar{0}}+E_{h+1,l}+\lambda|A^{\overline{1},-}_{h,k}-E_{h+1,l}}}])

\displaystyle-({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})\left[{(A+\lambda)_{h+1,k}+1}\atop 2\right]\big([{{A}^{\bar{0}}+2E_{h+1,k}-E_{h+1,l}+\lambda|A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,k}+E_{h+1,l}}]

\displaystyle-{[{(A+\lambda)_{h+1,l}}]_{{\upsilon}^{2}}}[{{A}^{\bar{0}}+2E_{h+1,k}+E_{h+1,l}+\lambda|A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,k}-E_{h+1,l}}]\big)\Big)\bigg\}.

Since

\tau_{h,k,l}({A^{\!\star}})=2(\overrightarrow{\bf r}^{l}_{h+1}-\overrightarrow{\bf r}_{h+1}^{k-1})+g^{-}_{h}(A,k)+{a}_{h+1,l},

where
\overrightarrow{\bf r}^{j}_{h+1}:=\overrightarrow{\bf r}^{j}_{h+1}(A)
, it follows that

(6.5.9)

\tau_{h,k,l}({A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}})=\tau_{h,k,l}({A^{\!\star}})+\varepsilon\lambda_{h}+\varepsilon^{\prime}\lambda_{h+1},

with
\varepsilon,\varepsilon^{\prime}\in\{-1,0,1\}
, depending on
h,k,l
.

Now we compute
(\clubsuit)_{h,k,l}
in two cases.

Case 1.
l\neq h+1
.
If
{a}^{\bar{1}}_{h+1,l}=0
, then
{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h+1,l}
,
A^{\overline{1},-}_{h,k}-E_{h+1,l}
and
A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,k}-E_{h+1,l}
all have a negative entry. Thus, noting
(A^{\bar{0}}+\lambda)_{h,k}{-}(A+\lambda)_{h,k}=-a^{\bar{1}}_{h,k}
,
(
6.5.8
) becomes

(6.5.10)

\displaystyle(\clubsuit)_{h,k,l}=(-1)^{\widetilde{a}^{\bar{1}}_{h,l}}{\upsilon}^{\tau_{h,k,l}({A^{\!\star}})}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\end{subarray}}{\upsilon}^{\lambda\centerdot(\varepsilon\epsilon_{h}+\varepsilon^{\prime}\epsilon_{h+1})}\big\{{\upsilon}^{-{a}^{\bar{1}}_{h,k}}[{(A^{\bar{0}}+\lambda)}_{h+1,k}+1]\cdot

\displaystyle[{A^{\overline{0},-}_{h,k}-E_{h+1,l}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h+1,l}}]+{\upsilon}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(A^{\bar{0}}+\lambda)}_{h,k}}[{{{A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h+1,l}+\lambda|A^{\overline{1},-}_{h,k}+E_{h+1,l}}}]

\displaystyle-({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1}){\upsilon}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(A^{\bar{0}}+\lambda)}_{h,k}}\left[{(A+\lambda)_{h+1,k}+1}\atop 2\right][{{A}^{\bar{0}}+2E_{h+1,k}-E_{h+1,l}+\lambda|A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,k}+E_{h+1,l}}]\big\}.

Replacing
{A^{\!\star}}
by
({A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h+1,l}|{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h+1,l})
and
(E_{h,h+1}|O)
by
(E_{h+1,h}|O)
when we write
\mathcal{Y}_{1},\mathcal{Y}_{2},\mathcal{Y}_{3}
as linear combinations of
{B^{\!\star}}(\bm{j},r)
with
{B^{\!\star}}\in M_{n}^{\pm}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})
and
\bm{j}\in\mathbb{Z}^{n}
in the proof of Proposition
5.2
(1), one proves that
(\clubsuit)_{h,k,l}
is a linear combination of
{M^{\!\star}}(\bm{j}^{\prime},r)
with
{M^{\!\star}}
of the form (
6.5.4
)

for
\dot{\delta}_{h+1,l}=1
.

Similarly,
if
{a}^{\bar{1}}_{h+1,l}=1
, then (
6.5.8
) becomes

(6.5.11)

\displaystyle(\clubsuit)_{h,k,l}=(-1)^{\widetilde{a}^{\bar{1}}_{h,l}}{\upsilon}^{\tau_{h,k,l}({A^{\!\star}})}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\end{subarray}}{\upsilon}^{\lambda\centerdot(\varepsilon\epsilon_{h}+\varepsilon^{\prime}\epsilon_{h+1})}[a_{h+1.l}]_{{\upsilon}^{2}}\cdot

\displaystyle\Big\{-{\upsilon}^{-{a}^{\bar{1}}_{h,k}}[{(A^{\bar{0}}+\lambda)}_{h+1,k}+1]\cdot[{A^{\overline{0},-}_{h,k}+E_{h+1,l}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h+1,l}}]

\displaystyle-{\upsilon}^{(A^{\bar{0}}+\lambda)_{h,k}}[{{{A}^{\bar{0}}+E_{h+1,l}+\lambda|A^{\overline{1},-}_{h,k}-E_{h+1,l}}}]

\displaystyle+({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1}){\upsilon}^{(A^{\bar{0}}+\lambda)_{h,k}}\left[{(A+\lambda)_{h+1,k}+1}\atop 2\right][{{A}^{\bar{0}}+2E_{h+1,k}+E_{h+1,l}+\lambda|A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,k}-E_{h+1,l}}]\Big\}.

Like the
{a}^{\bar{1}}_{h+1,l}=0
case,
(\clubsuit)_{h,k,l}=\text{a lin. comb. of }{M^{\!\star}}(\bm{j}^{\prime},r)
,
where
{M^{\!\star}}
is of the form (
6.5.4
) for
\dot{\delta}_{h+1,l}={1}
.

Case 2.
l=h+1
.

Since
l<k
,
(A+\lambda)_{h,k}=a_{h,k}
,
({A}^{\bar{0}}+\lambda)_{h,k}={a}^{\bar{0}}_{h,k}
,
(A+\lambda)_{h+1,k}=a_{h+1,k}
, and
(A+\lambda)_{h+1,l}=\lambda_{l}+{a}^{\bar{1}}_{h+1,l}
. Thus, in this case, (
6.5.8
) takes the form

\displaystyle(\clubsuit)_{h,k,l}

\displaystyle=(-1)^{\widetilde{a}^{\bar{1}}_{h,l}}~\cdot{\upsilon}^{\tau_{h,k,l}({A^{\!\star}})+a^{\bar{0}}_{h,k}}\cdot~\Big\{{\upsilon}^{-a_{h,k}}[{a}^{\bar{0}}_{h+1,k}+1]

\displaystyle\big\{\sum_{\lambda}{\upsilon}^{\lambda\centerdot(\varepsilon\epsilon_{h}+\varepsilon^{\prime}\epsilon_{h+1})}([{A^{\overline{0},-}_{h,k}-E_{h+1,h+1}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}}+E_{h+1,h+1}}]

\displaystyle-{[{\lambda}_{h+1}+1]_{{\upsilon}^{2}}}[{A^{\overline{0},-}_{h,k}+E_{h+1,h+1}+\lambda|{A}^{\bar{1}}-E_{h+1,h+1}}])\big\}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\big\{\sum_{\lambda}{\upsilon}^{\lambda\centerdot(\varepsilon\epsilon_{h}+\varepsilon^{\prime}\epsilon_{h+1})}([{{{A}^{\bar{0}}-E_{h+1,h+1}+\lambda|A^{\overline{1},-}_{h,k}+E_{h+1,h+1}}}]

\displaystyle-{[{\lambda}_{h+1}+1]_{{\upsilon}^{2}}}[{{{A}^{\bar{0}}+E_{h+1,h+1}+\lambda|A^{\overline{1},-}_{h,k}-E_{h+1,h+1}}}])\big\}-({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1})\left[{a_{h+1,k}+1}\atop 2\right]

\displaystyle\big\{\sum_{\lambda}{\upsilon}^{\lambda\centerdot(\varepsilon\epsilon_{h}+\varepsilon^{\prime}\epsilon_{h+1})}([{{A}^{\bar{0}}+2E_{h+1,k}-E_{h+1,h+1}+\lambda|A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,k}+E_{h+1,h+1}}]

\displaystyle-{[{\lambda}_{h+1}+1]_{{\upsilon}^{2}}}[{{A}^{\bar{0}}+2E_{h+1,k}+E_{h+1,h+1}+\lambda|A^{\overline{1},\approx}_{h,k}-E_{h+1,h+1}}])\big\}\Big\},

where
{\lambda}
runs over
\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)
.
Notice that all matrices appearing in above expression for
(\clubsuit)_{h,k,l}
have the form of
(\lambda+M^{\bar{0}}|M^{\bar{1}})
with
{M^{\!\star}}=(M^{\bar{0}}|M^{\bar{1}})
being the form of the matrices in (
6.5.4
) with
\dot{\delta}_{h+1,l}=0
.
Now, a similar argument to the proof of Proposition
5.3
(the
h=k
case) shows that
(\clubsuit)_{h,k,l}
has the required form.
This completes the proof of the claim and hence of (
6.5.7
).

On the other hand, by Corollary
6.4
, we see that
Y_{\overline{h},r}\cdot{{A^{\!\star}}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bm{0}},r)
is a linear combination of
{M^{\!\star}}(\bm{j},r)
with
{M^{\!\star}}\in M_{n}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})_{r}
and
\bm{j}\in\mathbb{Z}^{n}
. Further, by Proposition
4.3
(2), we may write
Y_{\overline{h},r}\cdot{{A^{\!\star}}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bm{0}},r)=\Sigma_{1}+\Sigma_{2}
,
where
\Sigma_{1}
(resp.,
\Sigma_{2}
) is a linear combination of
{M^{\!\star}}(\bm{j},r)
with
M={A}^{-}_{h,k}
(resp.,
M\prec{A}^{-}_{h,k}
for some
k
).
Meanwhile,
since every term
{B^{\!\star}}(\bm{j},r)
appearing in
{\rm{H}}\overline{\textsc{f}}(h,{A^{\!\star}}({\bf 0},r))
and
{\rm{HH}}\overline{\textsc{f}}(h,{A^{\!\star}}(\bm{0},r)
satisfies
B={A}^{-}_{h,k}
for some
k
with
1\leq k\leq n

by Proposition
5.5
and (
6.5.7
)
, equating the coefficients with respect to the standard basis
\{[{M^{\!\star}}]\}_{M^{\!\star}}
gives

\displaystyle\Sigma_{1}

\displaystyle={\rm{H}}\overline{\textsc{f}}(h,{A^{\!\star}}({\bf 0},r))+({\upsilon}-{\upsilon}^{-1}){\rm{HH}}\overline{\textsc{f}}(h,{A^{\!\star}}(\bm{0},r)),

\displaystyle\Sigma_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\\
\lambda\in\Lambda(n,r-|{A}|)\end{subarray}}\sum_{{B^{\!\star}}\in M_{n}(\mathbb{N}|{{\mathbb{N}}}_{2})_{r}\atop\exists k,B\prec{A}^{-}_{h,k}}{{\textsc{p}}_{{F^{\star}_{\bar{h},{\lambda}+\mathrm{ro}(A)}},A^{\star}_{\lambda},{B^{\!\star}}}}[{{B^{\!\star}}}].

This completes the proof of (
6.5.3
).
∎

7.
A triangular relation and its associated monomial basis

In this section, we shall establish a triangular relation (Theorem
7.13
) in the queer
{\upsilon}
-Schur superalgebra (cf.
[
BLM
, Lem. 3.8]
for
{\upsilon}
-Schur algebras and
[
DG
, Th. 7.4]
for
{\upsilon}
-Schur superalgebras). This is equivalent to
show that the transition matrix from a certain monomial basis to the standard basis is a triangular matrix.
To achieve this, we need to determine the leading term in each step of the multiplications in a monomial basis element
{\bf m}^{({A^{\!\star}})}
in Theorem
7.13
. There are three pairs of preparatory results: Propositions–Corollaries
7.4
–
7.5
,
7.6
–
7.7
, and
7.8
–
7.9
, serving this purpose.

For non-negative integers
k\leq l
and elements
{\bm{\mathrm{t}}}_{i},i\in[k,l]
in a ring, we make the following convention for the orders of products:

(7.0.1)
